     - `TrackerService.saveLap` MUST preserve its 4-argument and 5-argument method signatures (preserving test subclasses/callers) and keep `TrackerService.java <= 3,559` LoC (`3,538` baseline), resolving UTC `timeStart` via `MultisportLapCoordinator.formatUtcTimestamp(anchorMs)` when `anchorMs > 0L` (or `System.currentTimeMillis() - lapTime * 1000L` UTC fallback when `lapTime > 0`) and forwarding through `WorkoutRepository.saveLap(workoutId, lapNr, timeStart, lapTime, lapDistance, averageSpeed)`.
     - `LapsDatabaseManager.saveLap` MUST preserve its 5-arg, 6-arg, and 8-arg signatures; when invoked with `timeStart == null` and `lapTime > 0` by any legacy caller, it SHALL compute `System.currentTimeMillis() - (lapTime * 1000L)` formatted strictly in **UTC** `"yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss"` (never local timezone), ensuring `Laps.TIME_START` never silently records SQLite `CURRENT_TIMESTAMP` completion time while preserving `REQ-TRK-002` zero-duration/zero-distance rejection (`lapTime <= 0 && lapDistance <= 0.0`) and `NaN`/infinite speed sanitization.

**Requirement Archaeology & Chesterton's Fence Audit**:
1. *Original Requirement ID & Target*: Net-new `REQ-TRK-090`, extending `REQ-TRK-002` (`docs/requirements/req_tracking_cockpit_part1.md:281-307`) and `REQ-FIL-031.3` (`MultisportLapCoordinator.kt`); targets `MultisportLapCoordinator.kt`, `TrackerService.java`, `LapsDatabaseManager.java`, `WorkoutRepository.kt`.
2. *Historical Origin & Commit Trace*: `4fca7376` / `e7e02b0e` (`ATT-896`, `REQ-TRK-002` phantom-lap rejection & pause/resume neutrality); `5fb97345` (`ATT-8147`, `SYSREQ-TRK-003` linkage).
3. *Root Reason for Existing Formulation*: `REQ-TRK-002` preserved the 5-arg `saveLap` call chain and relied on SQLite `DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP` (`LapsDatabaseManager.java:558`), which inserts UTC write time (lap end) rather than lap start time for single-sport and final laps.
4. *Preservation of Core Invariants*: Preserves `REQ-TRK-002` zero-phantom rejection (`lapTime <= 0 && lapDistance <= 0.0`), pause/resume neutrality, 4-/5-arg `TrackerService.saveLap` and 5-arg `LapsDatabaseManager.saveLap` API compatibility, `TrackerService.java <= 3,559` LoC, `WorkoutRepository.kt <= 5,117` LoC, and canonical UTC `'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss'` formatting matching `FitFileWriter.parseUtcTimestamp()`.

**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:
• *Given* a tracked workout starting at `T0`, splitting Lap 1 at `T1` (`LAP_SUMMARY` `isPause=false`), and ending Lap 2 at `T2`,
• *When* `MultisportLapCoordinator` / `TrackerService.saveLap()` persists each lap (under any device timezone, e.g. `Europe/Berlin`),
• *Then* `Laps.TIME_START` stores `T0` for Lap 1 and `T1` for Lap 2 in UTC `'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss'` (captured pre-mutation before `currentLapStartAnchor` updates to `T1`), and `IS_PAUSE=true` does not overwrite `currentLapStartAnchor`.
• *Given* `LapsDatabaseManager.saveLap()` invoked with `timeStart == null` and `lapTime > 0` by any legacy caller,
• *When* persisted to `Laps.db`,
• *Then* `Laps.TIME_START` stores UTC `now - lapTime * 1000L` (`'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss'`) instead of SQLite `CURRENT_TIMESTAMP` end time, while `lapTime <= 0 && lapDistance <= 0.0` still rejects phantom laps.

**Invariants**: UTC `'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss'` matching `FitFileWriter.parseUtcTimestamp()` (never local TZ); `REQ-TRK-002` phantom-lap rejection & pause/resume neutrality; 4-/5-arg `TrackerService.saveLap` & 5-arg `LapsDatabaseManager.saveLap` signatures preserved; `TrackerService.java <= 3,559` LoC; `WorkoutRepository.kt <= 5,117` LoC; `Laps.db` `DB_VERSION = 3` unchanged; `0` new `src/main` reads (`REQ-PRO-049.1`); `Localization Parity: N/A - no UI strings`.
### REQ-FIL-038: Sensor Disconnect Filter Reset, Accumulator Preservation & Sparkline Flat-Line Termination (`ATT-8994`) [Verified]
- **Target Release**: `V5.0.0`
- **Tests**: `TST-FIL-038`
- **System Requirement(s)**: Derived (SW-only) — Internal sensor deactivation, `ProxySensor` unbinding, and `FilterManager` disconnect teardown lifecycle fix (refining `REQ-TRK-055` and `REQ-FIL-013`)
- **Priority**: Medium
- **Type**: Functional
- **Verification Method**: Unit, Integration
- **Implementation**: `MySensor.java`, `MyAccumulatorSensor.java`, `ProxySensor.java`, `FilterManager.kt`, `ANTHeartRateDevice.java`, `BTLEHeartRateDevice.java`, `PolarOnlineStreamingBridge.kt`
- **Rationale**: Disconnected device-pinned sensors (`Magene HR ANT+`, `Polar H10 ANT+`) left `InstantaneousFilter`/`MovingAverageFilter` tiles frozen on their last reading and fed horizontal flat-line samples into `LiveSparklineBuffer` because `MySensor.deactivateSensor()` did not notify listeners with `null`, HRM dynamic sensors were removed without deactivation, and `FilterManager.rebindExistingFilters()` ignored `currentSensor == null || !isActivated` (`ATT-8994`).
- **Description**:
  1. *Lock-Free Deactivation, Silent Accumulator Override & `ProxySensor` Safety (`REQ-FIL-038.1`)*: `MySensor.deactivateSensor()` SHALL set `mActivated = false`, clear `mValue = null`, and notify `mSensorListeners` (`CopyOnWriteArrayList`, lock-free) with `newValue(null)`. `MyAccumulatorSensor` and `ProxySensor` SHALL override `deactivateSensor()` to set `mActivated = false` silently without notifying `null`. `ProxySensor` SHALL track `mAccumulatorBacked` (`isAccumulatorBacked()`) whenever a `MyAccumulatorSensor` binds, and `ProxySensor.setSourceSensor(null)` SHALL notify `listenersSnapshot` with `newValue(null)` strictly outside `mSourceLock` (`REQ-SEN-021.2`) when `previousSource != null && !(previousSource instanceof MyAccumulatorSensor)`.
  2. *Dynamic Sensor Deactivation Before Removal (`REQ-FIL-038.2`)*: `ANTHeartRateDevice`, `BTLEHeartRateDevice`, and `PolarOnlineStreamingBridge` SHALL invoke `sensor.deactivateSensor()` prior to `unregisterSensor()`/`removeSensor()` for all dynamic sensors (`mRrIntervalSensor`, `mCadenceSensor`, `mStridesSensor`, `mVerticalOscillationSensor`, `mGroundContactTimeSensor`) across `unregisterSensors()`, `unregisterImuCadenceSensors()`, and `shutDown()`.
  3. *`FilterManager` Disconnect Clearing, Accumulator/Cumulative Immunity & `createFilter()` Parity (`REQ-FIL-038.3`)*: `FilterManager` SHALL track accumulator-backed filters explicitly via `mAccumulatorFilters` (populated when binding to `MyAccumulatorSensor` or accumulator-backed `ProxySensor`, never querying `MySensorManager.mAccumulatedSensorTypeSet`). In `rebindExistingFilters()` and `createFilter()`, unwrap `ProxySensor`: for device-pinned filters (`deviceName != null`) where `currentSensor == null || !currentSensor.isActivated`, unbind (`rebindFilter(filter, boundSensor, null)`) and clear non-accumulator live filters (`InstantaneousFilter` via `newValue(null)`; `MovingAverageFilter` via `reset()`) while preserving `AverageFilter`, `MaxValueFilter`, and `mAccumulatorFilters`, and defer new device-pinned inactive filters via `mRequiredButNotAvailableFilters`; for `"Bester Sensor"` (`deviceName == null`), keep bound to retained `ProxySensor` (`REQ-TRK-055.1`) and clear live non-accumulator filters only when `effectiveSource == null || !effectiveSource.isActivated`.
  4. *Sparkline Flat-Line Termination & Invariants (`REQ-FIL-038.4`)*: Clearing live filters to `null` (`"--"`) stops `BANALServiceRepository.updateSparklineBuffers()` from feeding frozen values into `LiveSparklineBuffer` with `0` edits to `BANALServiceRepository.kt`, `SensorFieldView.kt`, `DeviceManager.java`, `TrackerService.java`, and `DevicesDatabaseManager.java`; `Localization Parity: N/A - no new UI strings`.
- **Requirement Archaeology & Chesterton's Fence Audit**: Refines `REQ-TRK-055` (`ATT-6257`), `REQ-FIL-011` (`ATT-275`), `REQ-FIL-013` (`ATT-3222`), `REQ-SEN-021` (`ATT-4096`), `ATT-7530` (`c54768d2`); preserves lock-free callbacks, retained `ProxySensor` fallback, cumulative `AverageFilter`/`MaxValueFilter` & `MyAccumulatorSensor` session totals, `ExponentialSmoothingFilter` signature parity, and `0` protected monolith edits.
- **Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:
  • *Given* active non-accumulator & accumulator sensors, *When* `deactivateSensor()` or `ProxySensor.setSourceSensor(null)` runs, *Then* non-accumulator listeners receive `null` lock-free while `MyAccumulatorSensor` & `ProxySensor.deactivateSensor()` stay silent.
  • *Given* `ANTHeartRateDevice`/`BTLEHeartRateDevice`/`PolarOnlineStreamingBridge` dynamic sensors, *When* disconnecting/shutting down, *Then* `deactivateSensor()` runs before `unregisterSensor()`/`removeSensor()`, clearing `RR_INTERVAL`/IMU live tiles while keeping `STRIDES`.
  • *Given* device-pinned & `"Bester Sensor"` `InstantaneousFilter`, `TimedMovingAverageFilter`, `NumberedMovingAverageFilter`, `AverageFilter`, `MaxValueFilter`, and `STRIDES` filters, *When* sensors disconnect & repeated `SENSORS_CHANGED` fire, *Then* non-accumulator live filters clear to `null` (`"--"`) and sparkline feeding stops, cumulative/accumulator totals stay intact, `"Bester Sensor"` preserves fallback & `ProxySensor` binding, and reconnect rebinds cleanly.
### REQ-SEN-034: iTPMS Persisted Baseline Wiring, Live Reset Propagation & Strict Null GNSS Accuracy/Bearing Gating [Verified]
- **Target Release**: `V5.0.0`
- **Tests**: `TST-SEN-034`
- **System Requirement(s)**: `SYSREQ-TRK-004`, `SYSREQ-TRK-010`
- **Priority**: High
- **Type**: Functional
- **Verification Method**: Unit
- **Implementation**: `app/src/main/java/com/atrainingtracker/trainingtracker/repositories/BANALServiceRepository.kt`, `app/src/main/java/com/atrainingtracker/banalservice/ui/devices/devicedata/DeviceDataRepository.kt`
- **Rationale**: Follow-up defect from Ceremony 2 review of `ATT-4880`: `BANALServiceRepository` instantiated per-device `IndirectTpmsEstimator` instances with default `baselineCircumferenceMeters = 0.0` (never calling `DevicesDatabaseManager.getBaselineCircumference(devId)`), defaulted null GNSS accuracy/bearing to `5.0`/`0.0` (bypassing `<= 6.0 m` and `< 3.0 deg/s` gating), and gated GPS-free `DualWheelTpmsEstimator` inside `location != null` (`ATT-8421`, hardening `REQ-TRK-050`, `REQ-SEN-030.2`, and verifying `REQ-SEN-033.3`).
- **Description**:
  1. *Persisted `baselineCircumference` Loading, Live Propagation & Zero-Baseline Overwrite (`REQ-SEN-034.1`)*:
     - `BANALServiceRepository` SHALL load per-device `DevicesDatabaseManager.getBaselineCircumference(devId)` off the main thread (`Dispatchers.IO`, via an injectable baseline loader for JVM testing) into a `ConcurrentHashMap<Long, Double>` cache and apply the resolved baseline to both newly created and live cached `perDeviceItpmsEstimators[devId]` instances so `IndirectTpmsEstimator.TpmsAlertResult.LowPressure` fires when `C_start` drops `>= 0.6%` below a persisted baseline `> 0.0`.
     - Async `Dispatchers.IO` baseline loads, `DeviceDataRepository.baselineCircumferenceChanged` events, `BANALService.CALIBRATION_FACTOR_CHANGED` broadcasts (registered strictly via `registerInternalReceiver()` for JVM `SDK_INT == 0` safety), `clearBreadcrumbs()`, and `TrackingMode.READY -> TRACKING` reloads MUST push the resolved baseline onto live cached `perDeviceItpmsEstimators[devId]` instances, explicitly overwriting `baselineCircumferenceMeters = 0.0` when the persisted baseline is `<= 0.0` or non-finite.
     - `DeviceDataRepository` SHALL expose a buffered `SharedFlow<Long>` (`baselineCircumferenceChanged`, `extraBufferCapacity = 64`, `BufferOverflow.DROP_OLDEST`) emitted on both branches of `resetTirePressureBaseline()` (`circumferenceMm > 0` and `null`) and when `updateDevice()` modifies `baselineCircumference`.
  2. *Strict Null/Non-Finite GNSS Accuracy & Bearing Gating with GPS-Free Dual-Wheel Isolation (`REQ-SEN-034.2`)*:
     - `BANALServiceRepository` SHALL remove the synthetic fallback defaults `?: 5.0` (for `SensorType.ACCURACY`) and `?: 0.0` (for `_currentBearing.value`), skipping Section 1 (GNSS `IndirectTpmsEstimator`) whenever `location == null`, `acc == null || !acc.isFinite() || acc < 0.0`, or `bearing == null || !bearing.isFinite()`.
     - `BANALServiceRepository` SHALL scope `location != null` and GNSS `acc`/`bearing` guards strictly to Section 1 so Section 2 (GPS-free `DualWheelTpmsEstimator`, `REQ-SEN-030.1`) continues running under `TrackingMode.TRACKING` whenever `wheelDevices.size >= 2` even when GNSS `location`, `accuracy`, or `bearing` is `null` or non-finite.
  3. *Baseline Label 9-Locale Parity Regression & Architectural Guardrails (`REQ-SEN-034.3`)*:
     - `Localization Parity: N/A - already localized by ATT-8422 (00549967) via R.string.devices_wheel_baseline_format across all 9 active locales (values, de, es, fr, it, ja, nl, pl, pt)` with `0` remaining hardcoded English `"Baseline"` UI labels (regression guarded by `TranslationParityTest` & `EditDeviceWheelCircumferenceTest`).
     - `BANALServiceRepository.kt` (`<= 1,500` LoC) and `DeviceDataRepository.kt` (`<= 1,500` LoC) MUST respect file-size caps with `0` protected monolith edits (`DevicesDatabaseManager.java`, `EditDeviceDialog.kt`, `DeviceManager.java`, `TrackerService.java`), `0` SQLite schema bumps, and `0` `src/main` file reads in new tests (`REQ-PRO-049.1`).

**Requirement Archaeology & Chesterton's Fence Audit**:
1. *Original Requirement ID & Target*: Net-new `REQ-SEN-034`, hardening runtime wiring for `REQ-TRK-050` (`ATT-4880`), `REQ-SEN-030.2` (`ATT-7753`), and verifying `REQ-SEN-033.3` (`ATT-8422`).
2. *Historical Origin & Commit Trace*: `e7cc0f39` (`ATT-4880`), `a5665f9a` (`ATT-7753`), `00549967` (`ATT-8422`).
3. *Root Reason for Existing Formulation*: `ATT-4880` unit-tested `IndirectTpmsEstimator` directly while `BANALServiceRepository` kept default `IndirectTpmsEstimator()` and `?: 5.0` / `?: 0.0` fallbacks inside its 1-Hz loop; `ATT-7753` nested Section 2 inside `location != null`.
4. *Preservation of Core Invariants*: `IndirectTpmsEstimator` math/thresholds unchanged; `DualWheelTpmsEstimator` GPS-free behavior preserved; `persistConvergedCircumferences()`, `clearBreadcrumbs()`, `Devices.db` v8 schema, and `CALIBRATION_FACTOR` / wheel accumulators unchanged; `0` monolith edits; `0` `src/main` reads in new tests.

**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:
• *Given* persisted `baselineCircumference = 2.130 m` in `Devices.db`, *When* `BANALServiceRepository` creates/reloads the device's `IndirectTpmsEstimator` and `C_start` locks at `<= 2.115 m` (`-0.7%`), *Then* `TpmsAlertResult.LowPressure` fires; and *When* reset via `DeviceDataRepository` or cleared to `0.0` and reloaded, *Then* live cached estimators update immediately (including overwriting with `0.0`).
• *Given* `TrackingMode.TRACKING` with null/non-finite GNSS `accuracy`, `bearing`, or `location`, *When* `BANALServiceRepository` processes a tick, *Then* Section 1 (`IndirectTpmsEstimator`) skips the sample without fabricating `5.0`/`0.0`, while Section 2 (GPS-free `DualWheelTpmsEstimator`) continues detecting punctures uninterrupted.
• *Given* `ATT-8422` 9-locale `devices_wheel_baseline_format` parity and repository wiring changes, *When* targeted unit, localization, and contract suites run, *Then* all pass with `0` monolith growth and `0` new `src/main` reads.

**Invariants**: `IndirectTpmsEstimator` thresholds unchanged; GPS-free `DualWheelTpmsEstimator` active when GNSS is absent; `BANALServiceRepository.kt <= 1,500` LoC & `DeviceDataRepository.kt <= 1,500` LoC; `0` protected monolith edits; `0` SQLite schema bumps; `Localization Parity: N/A - already localized by ATT-8422`; `0` new `src/main` reads.

### REQ-CON-053: PolarOnlineStreamingBridge Atomic Stream Start Guards, PMD ERROR_ALREADY_IN_STATE Recovery & Blocked-Stream Warning (`ATT-9161`) [Verified]
- **Target Release**: `V5.0.0`
- **Tests**: `TST-CON-053`
- **System Requirement(s)**: `SYSREQ-TRK-001`, `SYSREQ-TRK-002`
- **Priority**: High
- **Type**: Functional
- **Verification Method**: Unit, Integration
- **Implementation**: `PolarPmdStreamCoordinator.kt`, `PolarOnlineStreamingBridge.kt`, `PolarPmdStreamWarningEffect.kt`, `TrackingTabsScreen.kt`, `DevicesTabbedScreen.kt`, `strings_devices.xml`
- **Rationale**: Overlapping Polar SDK handshake callbacks (`bleSdkFeatureReady`, `syncAlreadyOpenSessionIfReady`, `disInformationReceived`) re-entrantly cancel and restart unguarded `HR`, `ACC`, and `GYRO` coroutines (causing 7x `00002a37` CCCD toggle storms and overlapping PMD `REQUEST_MEASUREMENT_START` commands), while PMD `ERROR_ALREADY_IN_STATE` (from re-entrancy or background `fi.polar.polarflow`) silently kills `ACC`, `GYRO`, and `PPI` for the entire session with zero recovery or user feedback (`ATT-9161`).
- **Description**:
  1. *Unified 4-Stream Atomic Re-Entrancy Guard & Job-Identity Cleanup (`REQ-CON-053.1`)*: `PolarPmdStreamCoordinator` SHALL manage `HR`, `ACC`, `GYRO`, and `PPI` slots under a single lock with pre-launch atomic `compareAndSet(false, true)`, `CoroutineStart.LAZY` job publication, and `slot.job === myJob` identity-guarded `finally` cleanup. Duplicate start triggers while active/in-flight (including suspended `resolve*Setting` / `getAvailableOnlineStreamDataTypes`) SHALL be atomic no-ops; `ACC` SHALL claim its CAS slot BEFORE `tryAcquireAccChannel(..., ACC_OWNER_ONLINE)` and roll back cleanly if offline recording holds the channel.
  2. *Bounded PMD `ERROR_ALREADY_IN_STATE` Recovery (`REQ-CON-053.2`)*: Across `ACC`, `GYRO`, and `PPI`, classify `ERROR_ALREADY_IN_STATE` via typed `BleControlPointCommandError` (direct or `cause`) and message fallback; execute up to `3` bounded recovery attempts (`api.stopStreaming(sessionId, pmdType)` + injectable backoff `500 ms -> 1,000 ms -> 2,000 ms`) while connected and not shut down, resetting attempt count on first received frame and immediately rethrowing `CancellationException`.
  3. *Diagnostics, Session-Deduplicated Warning & 9-Locale Snackbar (`REQ-CON-053.3`)*: On retry exhaustion while connected, log structured diagnostics and emit ONE session-deduplicated `PolarPmdStreamWarning` onto `PolarPmdStreamCoordinator.streamWarning` (`StateFlow` with `consumeStreamWarning()` and reset on connect/disconnect/power-off/`shutDown()`), observed via shared `PolarPmdStreamWarningEffect(snackbarHostState)` in `TrackingTabsScreen.kt` & `DevicesTabbedScreen.kt` using 9-locale `polar_live_imu_stream_blocked_warning` in `strings_devices.xml`.
  4. *Monolith Ceilings & Regression Invariants (`REQ-CON-053.4`)*: Keep `PolarOnlineStreamingBridge.kt <= 1,306 / 1,500` LoC, `TrackingTabsScreen.kt <= 1,450 / 1,500` LoC, `0` edits to protected monoliths (`DeviceManager.java`, `TrackerService.java`, `DevicesDatabaseManager.java`), `0` upward `banalservice -> trainingtracker.ui` imports, and zero regression in `ATT-9176` (`REQ-HRV-051`) & `ATT-8994` (`REQ-FIL-038.2`).
- **Requirement Archaeology & Chesterton's Fence Audit**:
  1. *Original Requirement ID & Target*: Refines `REQ-SET-112.2`/`.3`, `REQ-CON-045.4`, `REQ-CON-050.2`/`.3`, `REQ-HRV-051.2`, preserving `REQ-FIL-038.2` in `PolarOnlineStreamingBridge.kt`.
  2. *Historical Origin & Commit Trace*: `ATT-8016` (`f4bef44c`), `ATT-8714` (`1e7a2f0b`), `ATT-8717` (`9f9e5967`), `ATT-9176` (`32ca4857`), `ATT-8994` (`001dd39f`).
  3. *Root Reason for Existing Formulation*: Prior iterations cancelled `hrJob`/`accJob`/`gyroJob` unconditionally assuming single-shot feature callbacks; `ATT-9176` added atomic CAS only for `PPI`, leaving `HR`/`ACC`/`GYRO` unguarded and all PMD streams without `ERROR_ALREADY_IN_STATE` recovery.
  4. *Preservation of Core Invariants*: Preserves single-GATT ref-counting (`REQ-SET-118`), `ACC` mutex (`REQ-SET-112.3`), `activeSdkStreamId` pinning (`REQ-CON-050.3`), `BSportType.RUN` gating, optical PPI H10 exclusion & `PolarPpiStreamProcessor`, dynamic sensor `deactivateSensor()`, and `0` protected monolith edits.
- **Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:
  • *Given* rapid overlapping SDK feature/DIS/session-sync callbacks on Polar H10 or Verity Sense, *When* streams start, *Then* at most one subscription runs per stream (`HR`, `ACC`, `GYRO`, `PPI`), `ACC` CAS rolls back if offline-locked, and cancelled jobs never clobber newer slots or `ACC_OWNER_ONLINE`.
  • *Given* `ACC`, `GYRO`, or `PPI` fails with `ERROR_ALREADY_IN_STATE`, *When* caught, *Then* `stopStreaming` + `500/1000/2000 ms` bounded retry (max `3`) recovers stream flow on success, or logs structured diagnostics and surfaces ONE session-deduplicated 9-locale Snackbar warning on exhaustion.

### REQ-CON-054: BLE HRM Silent-Connection Watchdog, Alias-Complete Unpair Teardown & Connected-GATT Rediscovery (`ATT-9155`) [Verified]
- **Target Release**: `V5.0.0`
- **Tests**: `TST-CON-054`
- **System Requirement(s)**: `SYSREQ-TRK-001`, `SYSREQ-TRK-002`
- **Priority**: High
- **Type**: Functional
- **Verification Method**: Unit, Integration
- **Implementation**: `BleHrmSilentConnectionWatchdog.kt`, `PolarOfflineGattCoordinator.kt`, `PolarOnlineStreamingBridge.kt`, `PolarPmdStreamCoordinator.kt`, `MyBTLEDevice.java`, `BTLEHeartRateDevice.java`, `BTSearchForNewDevicesEngine.java`, `DeviceDataRepository.kt`, `DeviceManager.java`
- **Rationale**: Field captures on Pixel 10 with Polar Verity Sense showed (1) BLE HRMs staying permanently connected with `--` when `0x2A37` CCCD or SDK HR collection stalls, and (2) unpairing/deleting leaving raw `BluetoothGatt` or `PolarBleApi` alias handles open so the peripheral stops advertising and stays invisible to BLE scans until power-cycled (`ATT-9155`).
- **Description**:
  1. *Alias-Complete Unpair/Delete Teardown (`REQ-CON-054.1`)*: `PolarOfflineGattCoordinator` SHALL provide `forceDisconnectDevice(context, mac, polarId)` and `disconnectUnpairedSessions(context, pairedProvider)` disconnecting all MAC/8-hex aliases and clearing `connectedDeviceIds`/`activeAccOwners`/`activeSessions` without harming sibling paired Polar sensors (`REQ-SET-118.3`). `PolarOnlineStreamingBridge.shutDown()`, `BTLEHeartRateDevice.shutDown()`, `DeviceDataRepository.updateDevice()`/`deleteDevice()` (capturing MAC pre-delete), and `DeviceManager.pairingChanged()` (even when absent from `mMyRemoteDevices`) SHALL invoke it; `MyBTLEDevice.disconnectFromGatt()` SHALL close synchronously when on `mHandler`'s looper and `onConnectionStateChange()` SHALL close late `STATE_CONNECTED` callbacks strictly when `mIsShutDown == true`.
  2. *Connected-GATT Discovery Fallback (`REQ-CON-054.2`)*: `BTSearchForNewDevicesEngine.startAsyncSearch()` SHALL release unpaired Polar SDK sessions for `HRM`, probe `BluetoothManager.getConnectedDevices(BluetoothProfile.GATT)` (permission-guarded, skipping already-paired DB sensors), and enforce target GATT service-UUID validation + immediate close of non-matching probes strictly on `mConnectedGattProbes`.
  3. *Silent-HR Watchdog & CCCD Retry (`REQ-CON-054.3`)*: Shared pure-JVM `BleHrmSilentConnectionWatchdog` (`12 s` default, injectable) SHALL drive both `BTLEHeartRateDevice` (preserving `REQ-CON-049` DIS-before-HR-CCCD order, retrying failed `0x2A37` CCCD status once, deferring while `isGattPaused()`) and `PolarOnlineStreamingBridge` to re-enable `0x2A37` / re-subscribe `HR` once on `12 s` silence, then cleanly disconnect/close & reconnect if still silent, disarming immediately on any HR packet.
  4. *Monolith & Governance Invariants (`REQ-CON-054.4`)*: Keep `DeviceManager.java <= 1,708` (`+0` net LoC = `1,707`), `0` edits to `TrackerService.java`/`DevicesDatabaseManager.java`, all edited Kotlin/Java files `<= 1,500` LoC, `0` new `src/main` reads, and `Localization Parity: N/A - no new or modified UI strings`.
- **Requirement Archaeology & Chesterton's Fence Audit**:
  1. *Original Requirement ID & Target*: Refines `REQ-CON-012`, `REQ-CON-018`, `REQ-CON-050`, `REQ-CON-053`, `REQ-SET-118.3` across `MyBTLEDevice`, `BTLEHeartRateDevice`, `BTSearchForNewDevicesEngine`, `PolarOfflineGattCoordinator`, `PolarOnlineStreamingBridge`.
  2. *Historical Origin & Commit Trace*: `ATT-1348`, `ATT-2224`, `ATT-8690`, `ATT-8714` (`1e7a2f0b`), `ATT-8717` (`9f9e5967`), `ATT-9161` (`104a028f`).
  3. *Root Reason for Existing Formulation*: Prior teardown assumed single-ID `mMyRemoteDevices` ownership, scans assumed unpaired peripherals always advertise, and HR CCCD writes were fire-and-forget.
  4. *Preservation of Core Invariants*: Preserves `REQ-CON-049` DIS-before-CCCD order (`BTLEHeartRateDeviceTest:561`), `MyBTLEDeviceLifecycleTest:323` non-shutdown null-GATT callback, `REQ-CON-050.1` ordered teardown, `REQ-SET-118.3` sibling Polar session isolation, `REQ-CON-053` 4-stream coordination, and `DeviceManager.java <= 1,708` LoC.
- **Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:
  • *Given* a connected BLE/Polar HRM (optionally alongside a sibling paired Polar sensor), *When* unpaired or deleted, *Then* both raw `BluetoothGatt` and all MAC/8-hex `PolarBleApi` aliases tear down immediately without affecting the sibling sensor.
  • *Given* an unpaired/deleted compatible BLE HRM still GATT-connected to the phone, *When* BLE search starts, *Then* unpaired Polar sessions release and `getConnectedDevices(GATT)` probes surface the sensor while skipping already-paired and non-matching peripherals.
  • *Given* a connected BLE HRM receiving `0` HR packets for `12 s` (and not GATT-paused), *When* the watchdog fires, *Then* `0x2A37` CCCD / `HR` stream re-enables once and, if still silent, cleanly disconnects/closes and reconnects.

### REQ-TRK-091: Decoupled Stop-Workout Post-Processing, Short-Service Progress Notification & Edit-Safe Refresh (`ATT-9154`) [Verified]
- **Target Release**: `V5.0.0`
- **Tests**: `TST-TRK-091`
- **System Requirement(s)**: `SYSREQ-TRK-005`, `SYSREQ-DEX-007`
- **Priority**: High
- **Type**: Functional
- **Verification Method**: Unit, Integration
- **Implementation**: `WorkoutPostProcessingCoordinator.kt`, `WorkoutPostProcessingService.kt`, `WorkoutPostProcessingNotificationManager.kt`, `TrackerService.java`, `WorkoutRepository.kt`, `EditWorkoutViewModel.kt`, `PolarH10OfflineController.kt`, `PolarVeritySenseOfflineController.kt`, `AndroidManifest.xml`, `strings.xml`
- **Rationale**: Connected armed Polar H10/Verity Sense offline backfill and HRV/ERTS recomputation synchronously block `TrackerService.endWorkout()` before `TRACKING_FINISHED_INTENT`, stalling Stop -> `EditWorkoutScreen` navigation (`ATT-9154`).
- **Description**:
  1. *Non-Blocking Stop & Armed-Token Isolation (`REQ-TRK-091.1`)*: `TrackerService.endWorkout()` SHALL commit `FINISHED = 1`, persist devices/RR/sensors/metadata, run `finalizeLiveSession()`, close `PolarRawTelemetryRecorder.stopActiveSession()`, capture armed flags/start timestamps/monotonic armed tokens synchronously, enqueue `WorkoutPostProcessingCoordinator`, and broadcast `TRACKING_FINISHED_INTENT` immediately (`< 1 s`). Async backfill SHALL run sequentially (`H10(showOwnNotification=false)` -> `Verity(showOwnNotification=false)` -> `HrvPostWorkoutRecomputer` -> optional `ErtsPostWorkoutRecomputer` -> `refreshWorkout` -> `ExportManager.workoutFinished` -> deferred `exportWorkout`) with per-stage fault isolation, clearing `isArmed` strictly when the armed token still matches the stopping workout.
  2. *Atomic Export Deferral Lock & Edit-Safe Refresh (`REQ-TRK-091.2`)*: `WorkoutPostProcessingCoordinator` SHALL coordinate `activeWorkouts` and `deferredExportWorkouts` under a single lock so early `WorkoutRepository.saveWorkout()` persists metadata + updates `_allWorkouts` (incl. `rpe`/`feel`) immediately while deferring `exportManager.exportWorkout(freshWorkoutFromDb)` until post-processing completes. `EditWorkoutViewModel` SHALL observe `repository.allWorkouts` filtered to `workoutId` and merge strictly telemetry/derived fields without overwriting user-editable fields or re-running sport/equipment suggestion rewrites.
  3. *`shortService` FGS, Progress Notification `8047`/`8048` & 9-Locale Parity (`REQ-TRK-091.3`)*: Declare `WorkoutPostProcessingService` with `android:foregroundServiceType="shortService"` (`0` new manifest permissions, `0` `connectedDevice`), API 34+ `onTimeout()` clean stop, and `ForegroundServiceStartNotAllowedException` in-process fallback. `WorkoutPostProcessingNotificationManager` (`ID 8047` progress, `ID 8048` warning, `NOTIFICATION_CHANNEL__EXPORT`, API 33+ `POST_NOTIFICATIONS` guard) SHALL update localized stage progress across all 9 locales (`0` emojis), cancel `8047` on completion, and cancel `8047` + post an auto-cancel warning on dedicated ID `8048` on failure so stopping the FGS never wipes the warning.
  4. *LoC & Governance Invariants (`REQ-TRK-091.4`)*: `TrackerService.java < 3,539 / 3,559`, `WorkoutRepository.kt <= 5,117`, `PolarVeritySenseOfflineController.kt <= 1,500`, `0` edits to `DeviceManager.java`/`DevicesDatabaseManager.java`, `0` schema migrations, `0` new `src/main` reads.
- **Requirement Archaeology & Chesterton's Fence Audit**:
  1. *Original Requirement ID & Target*: Refines `REQ-SET-105.2`/`.5`, `REQ-SET-109.1`, preserving `REQ-TRK-022`, `REQ-TRK-030`, `REQ-TRK-033`, `REQ-SET-119`, `REQ-UI-211`.
  2. *Historical Origin & Commit Trace*: `ATT-3986`/`ATT-4099` (`aa0e9097`), `ATT-3567`, `ATT-7918` (`60ec5c3b`), `ATT-7924`, `ATT-8969` (`8e44039d`).
  3. *Root Reason for Existing Formulation*: Synchronous `endWorkout()` ordering ensured Polar backfill and HRV/ERTS completed before `ExportManager` export generation.
  4. *Preservation of Core Invariants*: Preserves isolated `FINISHED = 1` commit first, zero-gap Polar fast paths & `5 s` connect timeout / disconnected fast-fail, Verity `WorkoutRr.db` isolation, export-after-backfill+ERTS ordering, no `connectedDevice`, and hidden-field preservation.
- **Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:
  • *Given* Polar H10/Verity offline recording is armed, *When* Stop is tapped, *Then* `TRACKING_FINISHED_INTENT` fires in `< 1 s`, async stages run with unified progress notification `8047`, back-to-back armed tokens are isolated, early user edits/saves never lose data or export stale telemetry, and `shortService` + 9-locale parity hold.

### REQ-FIL-039: PhoneCarriageClassifier Front-Hip Belt Discrimination, Viewing-Inclination Glance Guard & Active-Footstrike Cycling Mask (`ATT-9347`) [Verified]
- **Target Release**: `V5.0.0`
- **Tests**: `TST-FIL-039`
- **System Requirement(s)**: `SYSREQ-TRK-003`, `SYSREQ-TRK-004`, `SYSREQ-TRK-010`
- **Priority**: Medium
- **Type**: Functional
- **Verification Method**: Unit
- **Implementation**: `app/src/main/java/com/atrainingtracker/banalservice/odometry/PhoneCarriageClassifier.kt`
- **Rationale**: On Pixel 10 run `Kurze Runde var 2 #54` (`2026-11-20_173833`, `_id = 2422`, `Sport = RUN`, 100% Salomon front-hip running belt, zero cycling sensors), `ATT-9157` improved `RUN_WAIST_HIP` (`0.3% -> 47.3%`) yet `RUN_HANDHELD (7)` still claimed `42.5%` (`1,164 s`, `111` transitions) due to steep front-hip belt cliffs (`swingSd 15.5°`, `yaw 0.68`, `baro 0.45`), false vertical-plane belt-slip glances (`>= 15°` without screen-viewing tilt), and symmetric `6 s` dwell, while `BIKE_FRONT_POCKET (4)` still bled `29 s` (`1.1%`) when post-crash `UNKNOWN` sport hint or `>= 5.5 m/s` GNSS spikes lifted `suppressCyclingForRun` despite active running footstrikes (`ATT-9347`, refining `REQ-FIL-028.1`, `REQ-FIL-034.2`/`.4`, `REQ-FIL-035.1`–`.3`).
- **Description**:
  1. *Active-Footstrike Cycling Mask & High-Impact Step-Batching Guard (`REQ-FIL-039.1`)*: `PhoneCarriageClassifier.evaluateRawCandidate()` SHALL mask `BIKE_HANDLEBAR`, `BIKE_JERSEY_BACK`, and `BIKE_FRONT_POCKET` (`Double.NEGATIVE_INFINITY`, including zero-IMU fallback and stale `BIKE_*` `committedScore = -Inf`) whenever `suppressCyclingForRun` holds OR `suppressCyclingForFootstrikes` holds (`!wheelActiveMoving && (wheelSpeed == null || wheelSpeed <= 0.5) && effectiveRunStepFreqHz != null && effectiveRunStepFreqHz >= 2.25`). Furthermore, `stepDetectorActiveAndZero` and `evaluateAccelFootstrike()` MUST NOT set `confirmedNoFootstrikes = true` when `accelEval.impactRmsMps2 >= 1.8 m/s²`, preventing SensorHub step-batching gaps on high-impact trail running from awarding `cyclingPrior += 1.5` while preserving uphill MTB (`impactRms < 1.2 m/s²`) and triathlon/brick overrides (`wheel > 0.5` or `speed >= 5.5` with zero footstrikes).
  2. *Viewing-Inclination Glance Guard (`REQ-FIL-039.2`)*: `PhoneCarriageClassifier.detectGlanceShift()` SHALL compute recent/previous gravity inclinations (`acos(|mz|)`) and MUST reject any orientation shift where `recInclinationDeg > 60.0°` OR `(prevInclinationDeg - recInclinationDeg) < 10.0°`, eliminating false vertical-plane belt-roll/slip and hill-lean glances (including large `>= 30°` lateral vertical rolls) while preserving genuine wrist glances (`70–72° -> 18–35°`, `55° -> 30°`) and `ATT-9062` cyclic thigh-flexion rejection.
  3. *Calibrated Front-Hip Belt Scoring & Asymmetric Non-Glance Dwell (`REQ-FIL-039.3`)*: `scoreRunWaistHip()` (`swingSd < 10.0 -> +2.6`, `< 16.0 -> +2.0`, `< 18.0 -> +0.8`, `< 22.0 -> -0.5`, else `-2.0`; `yaw < 0.06 -> +0.8`, `<= 0.48 -> +2.2`, `<= 0.85 -> +1.8`, `<= 1.15 -> +0.4`, else `-1.2`; `baro < 0.45 -> +1.2`, `< 0.60 -> +0.5`, else `-0.6`; `step >= 2.15 -> +0.8`; `glance == 0 -> +1.4`, else `-1.8`), `scoreRunBicepArm()` (`yaw < 0.16 -> -1.0`, `<= 0.48 -> +2.0`, `<= 0.60 -> +0.3`, else `-0.8`; `glance == 0 -> +0.8`, else `-2.2`), and `scoreRunHandheld()` (`swingSd >= 17.5 -> +1.8`, `>= 13.5 -> +0.4`, else `-1.6`; `yaw >= 0.72 -> +2.4`, `>= 0.55 -> +0.6`, else `-1.5`; `baro >= 0.60 -> +2.4`, `>= 0.45 -> +0.4`, else `-0.4`; `glance >= 1 -> +3.8`, `== 0 -> -1.8`) SHALL classify continuous front-hip belt running predominantly (`>= 85%`) as `RUN_WAIST_HIP` while preserving winning margins for rear-clip, bicep armband (`17°`), unglanced handheld (`18.4°`/`0.74`/`0.83`), and glanced handheld (`SpeedAndLocationDevice_ATTFusedTest:837, 865`, `yaw = 0`). `requiredDwellMs()` SHALL enforce `maxOf(mountHysteresisMs, 10_000L)` for non-glance `RUN_WAIST_HIP -> RUN_HANDHELD` while keeping `0 ms` verified glance, `4 s` `RUN -> WALK`, `6 s` general intra-run, and `2 s` `WALK -> RUN` / `STATIONARY`.
- **Requirement Archaeology & Chesterton's Fence Audit**:
  1. *Original Requirement ID & Target*: Net-new `REQ-FIL-039` refining `REQ-FIL-028.1`, `REQ-FIL-034.2`/`.4`, and `REQ-FIL-035.1`–`.3` strictly inside `PhoneCarriageClassifier.kt`.
  2. *Historical Origin & Commit Trace*: `fee3e805` (`ATT-8890`), `0210b827` (`ATT-9062`), `6d80345c` (`ATT-9157`).
  3. *Root Reason for Existing Formulation*: `ATT-9157` calibrated against synthetic `swingSd <= 14.5°` / `yaw <= 0.60` / `baro <= 0.45` envelopes and `sportHint == RUNNING && speed < 5.5` before run `2422` revealed wider Salomon belt dynamics, vertical-plane belt slips, and post-crash `UNKNOWN` sport-hint fallback.
  4. *Preservation of Core Invariants*: Pure-JVM determinism; `PhoneCarriageMode` codes (`-1..7`) & `CarriageFilterProfile` immutable; `0` edits to `SpeedAndLocationDevice_ATTFused.kt` (`1,489/1,500`) & protected monoliths; all 9 carriage modes, uphill MTB (`impactRms < 1.2`), brick overrides, and `ATT-9062`/`ATT-9157` tests preserved; `0` DB schema bumps; `0` `src/main` test reads; `Localization Parity: N/A - no UI strings`.
- **Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:
  • *Given* `Sport = RUN` without a cycling speed sensor (`< 5.5 m/s`), OR `sportHint == UNKNOWN` / transient `>= 5.5 m/s` GNSS spike with active running footstrikes (`>= 2.25 Hz`) or high-impact trail step-batching gaps (`impactRms >= 1.8 m/s²`), *When* `PhoneCarriageClassifier` scores candidates, *Then* `BIKE_FRONT_POCKET` and all `BIKE_*` modes record `0%` (`0 s`), while uphill MTB (`impactRms < 1.2`) and footstrike-free brick transitions (`wheel > 0.5` or `speed >= 5.5`) still classify `BIKE_*`.
  • *Given* a 45-min synthetic front-hip running belt session (`155–170 spm`, `swingSd 10–17.5°`, `yaw 0.45–1.05 rad/s`, `baro 0.25–0.58 m/s`, vertical belt rolls/slips `18–35°` at `recIncl > 60°`, plus obstacle walk and genuine wrist glance), *When* `PhoneCarriageClassifier` classifies, *Then* steady running classifies `>= 85%` as `RUN_WAIST_HIP` with zero false vertical glances, drastically reduced transitions (`<< 111`), and intact detection of obstacle walks (`4 s`), wrist glances (`0 ms`), bicep armband, unglanced handheld, and post-glance handheld (`yaw = 0`).

### REQ-TRK-093: Polar H10 Exact Recording Time Anchor, Workout-End RR Trimming, Single-Pass Post-Backfill Physiology Recomputation & Legacy GATT Release [Verified]
- **Target Release**: `V5.0.0`
- **Tests**: `TST-TRK-093`
- **System Requirement(s)**: `SYSREQ-TRK-004`, `SYSREQ-TRK-005`
- **Priority**: High
- **Type**: Functional
- **Verification Method**: Unit
- **Implementation**: `PolarH10OfflineController.kt`, `PostWorkoutPhysiologyRecomputer.kt`, `WorkoutPostProcessingCoordinator.kt`, `TrackerService.java`, `MyBTLEDevice.java`
- **Rationale**: Eliminates 3–15 s BLE arming drift on Polar H10 offline RR/HR backfill, trims trailing post-workout cool-down RR beats, ensures Efficiency Factor (EF), Aerobic Decoupling, `PowerHrDynamicsCalculator`, HR summary extrema, and `HrvPostWorkoutRecomputer` each run strictly once on the gap-filled series without blocking `mDbExecutor`, and releases competing legacy `BluetoothGatt` handles during Polar PMD/PFTP transfers while locking existing `RESUME_SERVICE_RECREATION` (`REQ-TRK-078.1`) and `LAP_NR` (`REQ-STB-025.1`) invariants (`ATT-8124`).
- **Description**:
  1. *Exact `startRecording` Time Anchor & Lifecycle (`REQ-TRK-093.1`)*: `PolarH10OfflineController.executeArming()` SHALL capture `recordingStartTimeMs` via an injectable `clockMsProvider` strictly after `api.startRecording(...)` completes and store it under `armedStateLock`; `recordingStartTimeMs` SHALL be cleared to `0L` in `clearArmedStateIfTokenMatches()` (when token matches), `resetForTesting()`, and `setSessionStartTimeMs()` when unarmed. `getEffectiveAnchorStartTimeMs()` SHALL return `recordingStartTimeMs` when `> 0L`, else `sessionStartTimeMs`, serving as the default `startTimeMs` for `stopAndBackfillWorkout()`, `stopAndBackfillWorkoutBlocking()`, `TrackerService.endWorkout()`, and `recordPendingSync()`.
  2. *Workout-End RR Trimming & Clock-Inversion Guard (`REQ-TRK-093.2`)*: `TrackerService.endWorkout()` SHALL pass `workoutEndMs` into `WorkoutPostProcessingCoordinator.PostProcessingRequest` (`@JvmOverloads`, default `0L`), forwarded to `stopAndBackfillWorkout(..., endTimeMs)` and `recordPendingSync(..., endTimeMs)` (persisting `if (endTimeMs > 0L) endTimeMs else clockMsProvider()`). Shared helper `buildAnchoredSamples(workoutId, hrSamples, anchorMs, endTimeMs)` (used by `stopAndBackfillWorkout()` and `syncPendingWorkout()` with `pendingData.expectedEndMs`) SHALL skip `rr <= 0` before accumulation/division, skip trimming when `endTimeMs <= 0L`, log a warning without dropping all beats on clock inversion (`0 < endTimeMs < anchorMs`), and `break` once `anchorMs + cumulativeMs > endTimeMs` when `endTimeMs >= anchorMs`; callers SHALL guard `intervals.isNotEmpty()` before DB patching.
  3. *Single-Pass Post-Backfill Physiology & Consistent `h10Armed` Snapshot (`REQ-TRK-093.3`)*: `PostWorkoutPhysiologyRecomputer.recomputeAfterHrBackfill(context, workoutId, baseFileName)` SHALL filter nullable/non-finite/non-positive `hrSamples` to persist HR `MIN`/`MAX`/`AVG` (with valid count), resolve `BSportType` via `WorkoutRepository.getWorkoutSummaryUpdateData(baseFileName)?.bSportType ?: BSportType.UNKNOWN`, and persist `EFFICIENCY_FACTOR`, `AEROBIC_DECOUPLING`, and `CardioPowerDynamicsRecord`. `TrackerService.endWorkout()` SHALL capture `h10Armed` once before `finalizeLiveSession()` and reuse that snapshot for both deferral and `PostProcessingRequest`. `WorkoutPostProcessingCoordinator` SHALL pass `runPostBackfillRecompute = false` to `stopAndBackfillWorkout()` and execute `runPhysiologyRecompute` (on `h10Armed || verityBackfilled`) and `runHrvRecompute` strictly once before `reloadWorkout` and export. `syncPendingWorkout()` and direct `runPostBackfillRecompute = true` calls SHALL run both recomputers once post-patch.
  4. *Synchronous Legacy GATT Release, Sensor Re-Registration & Crash-Recovery/`LAP_NR` Lock (`REQ-TRK-093.4`)*: `MyBTLEDevice.pauseGattOperations()` SHALL set `mIsGattPaused = true`, `mState = State.DISCONNECTED`, and null `mBluetoothGatt` under `mGattLock` while preserving `mIsBackgroundReconnecting` (`REQ-CON-051.3`), then outside `mGattLock` cancel scan/timers, invoke `unregisterSensors()` when tearing down an active connection (ensuring `readNextCharacteristic()` invokes `configureServices(gatt)` after reconnect), and synchronously disconnect/close `gattToClose` (`BLUETOOTH_CONNECT`-guarded). `onConnectionStateChange()` SHALL close any stale `gatt != currentGatt` outside `mGattLock` and return immediately while `mIsGattPaused == true`. `RESUME_SERVICE_RECREATION` (`REQ-TRK-078.1`), `LAP_NR` (`REQ-STB-025.1`), and `TST-CON-054.3` invariants remain verified.

**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:
• *Given* Polar H10 arming executes 8 s after `sessionStartTimeMs` and strap recording continues past `workoutEndMs`, *When* `stopAndBackfillWorkout()` or `syncPendingWorkout()` runs, *Then* Beat 0 anchors to `recordingStartTimeMs` and beats with `beatTs > workoutEndMs` (and `rr <= 0`) are excluded.
• *Given* an armed Polar H10 workout with live HR gaps and a connected legacy `MyBTLEDevice`, *When* tracking stops, *Then* `pauseGattOperations()` closes `mBluetoothGatt` (preserving `mIsBackgroundReconnecting`), H10 backfill patches HR/RR off `mDbExecutor` before `PostWorkoutPhysiologyRecomputer` and `HrvPostWorkoutRecomputer` each run once, followed by `reloadWorkout` and export.
• *Given* `RESUME_SERVICE_RECREATION` and a fresh `MyAccumulatorSensor` (`zeroValue = 1`), *When* restored/constructed, *Then* zero `RESET_ACCUMULATORS_INTENT` broadcasts occur and `LAP_NR` starts deterministically at `1`.

**Invariants**: `TrackerService.java <= 3,559` LoC; `MyBTLEDevice.java <= 1,500` LoC; `ATT-6178` non-blocking `onDestroy()` intact; `ATT-9154` `< 1 s` stop & deferred-export lock intact; `TST-CON-051.3` passive reconnect intact; zero SQLite schema changes; `Localization Parity: N/A - no UI strings`.

### REQ-TRK-094: Deduplicated TrackerService Shutdown & Type-Safe Lap Extraction, PostWorkoutFinalizer Extraction, Order-Preserving TrainingApplication Teardown & Parameterized Strava Sync [Verified]
- **Target Release**: `V5.0.0`
- **Tests**: `TST-TRK-094`
- **System Requirement(s)**: `SYSREQ-TRK-004`, `SYSREQ-TRK-005`
- **Priority**: Medium
- **Type**: Functional
- **Verification Method**: Unit
- **Implementation**: `TrackerService.java`, `PostWorkoutFinalizer.kt`, `LapTelemetryExtractor.kt`, `BANALService.java`, `TrainingApplication.java`, `StravaSyncScheduler.kt`, `StravaEquipmentSyncWorker.kt`, `StravaRoutesSyncWorker.kt`, `StravaSegmentsSyncWorker.kt`
- **Rationale**: Eliminates duplicated shutdown/finalization boilerplate, unsafe raw lap casts (`ClassCastException` hazard), mixed post-workout finalization responsibilities in `TrackerService.java`, duplicated teardown resets in `TrainingApplication.java`, and copy-pasted Strava sync preferences/worker scheduling while ratcheting monolith LoC downward (`ATT-6034`).
- **Description**:
  1. *Parameterized Shutdown & `Number`-Safe Lap Extraction (`REQ-TRK-094.1`)*: `TrackerService.handleActionStop()` and `onDestroy()` SHALL share parameterized controller-stop and async `mDbExecutor` finalization helpers while preserving `ATT-6178` non-blocking `onDestroy()` (`REQ-TRK-034.4`), `mTableInitializationFuture` cancellation, `sInstance` reset, `mRadarAlertManager` release, executor shutdown deferral (`REQ-TRK-076.1`), and `mSessionGeneration`-gated teardown (`REQ-TRK-076.3`). `LapTelemetryExtractor.extract(...)` SHALL replace all 3 lap extraction sites (`BanalFinalizationSnapshot.capture`, `TrackerService.createNewLap`, `BANALService.newLap`) using `instanceof Number` + finiteness checks and safe default display strings (`"??:??:??"`, `"0"`).
  2. *Extracted `PostWorkoutFinalizer` (`REQ-TRK-094.2`)*: `PostWorkoutFinalizer` (`com.atrainingtracker.trainingtracker.tracker`) SHALL encapsulate the 7 fault-isolated `finalizeLiveSession` stages and `guessCommuteAndTrainer(repository, workoutId)` using strictly repository abstractions (zero `*DatabaseManager`/`Cursor` access); `TrackerService` SHALL preserve all existing protected `finalizeLiveSession` (0-/1-/4-arg) and `guessCommuteAndTrainer` (0-/1-arg) signatures as delegates.
  3. *Order-Preserving `TrainingApplication` Teardown Helpers (`REQ-TRK-094.3`)*: `TrainingApplication` SHALL extract `private static` `resetPauseLocks(@Nullable Context)` and `resetActiveTrackingState(@Nullable Context, boolean)` used across `stopTracking()`, `resetTrackingStateOnAbort()`, and `onTrackingAborted()`, preserving `stopTracking()`'s pre/post-dispatch reset ordering and leaving `trackingStopped()` semantically untouched.
  4. *Parameterized Strava Sync Preferences & Worker Scheduling (`REQ-TRK-094.4`)*: `TrainingApplication` SHALL back all 18 public static Strava `Equipment`/`Routes`/`Segments` preference methods via a parameterized `StravaSyncPref` enum (retaining per-entity `"never"` string resources and null `cSharedPreferences`/`cAppContext` guards), `StravaSyncScheduler.schedulePeriodicSync(...)` SHALL deduplicate `schedule(context)` across all 3 `Strava*SyncWorker` classes preserving exact `TAG`/`WORK_NAME`/constraints, and `ArchitectureConformanceContractTest` SHALL ratchet down `TrackerService.java` and `TrainingApplication.java` LoC ceilings.

**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:
• *Given* `Number` subtypes, `NaN`, non-numeric values, or `null` in lap `SensorData`, *When* `LapTelemetryExtractor.extract(...)` runs, *Then* zero `ClassCastException` occurs and `handleActionStop()`/`onDestroy()` preserve all `ATT-6178`/`REQ-TRK-033`/`034`/`076` invariants.
• *Given* a `LiveWorkoutSession` with telemetry/streams and `deferHrPhysiologyToPostProcessing` `true`/`false`, *When* `PostWorkoutFinalizer.finalizeLiveSession(...)` runs, *Then* all 7 stages persist identical metrics with per-stage fault isolation and return `MilestoneCelebrationData`.
• *Given* `TrainingApplication` teardown and Strava sync scheduling, *When* stopping/aborting tracking or scheduling Strava workers, *Then* `stopTracking()` preserves pre/post-dispatch ordering, `trackingStopped()` stays untouched, all 18 Strava pref APIs preserve keys/`"never"` defaults, and all 3 workers preserve exact `TAG`/`WORK_NAME`/constraints.

**Invariants**: `TrackerService.java <= 3,220` LoC; `TrainingApplication.java <= 2,050` LoC; `ATT-6178`/`ATT-9154`/`ATT-8124` contracts intact; zero SQLite schema or UI/localization changes (`N/A - no UI strings`).

### REQ-TRK-095: Race-Free Foreground Service Startup/Teardown Handshake for `TrackerService` and `WorkoutPostProcessingService` (`ATT-9498`) [Verified]
- **Target Release**: `V5.0.0`
- **Tests**: `TST-TRK-095`
- **System Requirement(s)**: `SYSREQ-TRK-005`, `SYSREQ-TRK-013`
- **Priority**: Critical
- **Type**: Functional
- **Verification Method**: Unit, Integration
- **Implementation**: `TrackerService.java`, `TrainingApplication.java`, `WorkoutPostProcessingService.kt`, `WorkoutPostProcessingCoordinator.kt`, `MultiLocaleUiSweepAndroidTest.kt`, `docs/architecture/detailed_design/tracker_service.md`
- **Rationale**: KW47 weekly emulator sweep (`docs/engineering/emulator_sweeps/2026-11-21_sweep.md`, Finding M11) crashed in `7/12` runs on Android 12/15 with `ForegroundServiceDidNotStartInTimeException` because rapid stop calls invoked `stopService()` on `TrackerService` or `WorkoutPostProcessingService` while an asynchronous `ContextCompat.startForegroundService()` request had not yet executed `Service.startForeground()`, and rapid `ACTION_STOP` before async `createNewWorkout()` completed left orphan `FINISHED = 0` workouts (`ATT-9498`).
- **Description**:
  1. *`TrackerService` Pending-FGS Handshake, Early Promotion & FIFO `mWorkoutID` Resolution (`REQ-TRK-095.1`)*: `TrainingApplication.startTracking()` SHALL call `TrackerService.markForegroundStartRequested()` upon successful `ContextCompat.startForegroundService(this, intent)`, and `TrainingApplication.stopTracking()` when `!TrackerService.isRunning()` SHALL invoke `TrackerService.requestStopIfForegroundStartPending()` and suppress `stopService(TrackerService)` while `true` (retaining `stopService()` strictly when idle). `TrackerService.onStartCommand()` SHALL invoke `performStartForeground(...)` prior to `mDbExecutor.submit(...)` / `mScheduler` (preserving the `intent == null && !hasBackgroundLocationPermission()` early abort without `startForeground()`), tear down cleanly without creating an orphan DB row when a pre-start stop was queued, satisfy any pending unpromoted FGS start in `handleActionStop()` before early `performStopSelf()`, reset `mWorkoutID = -1` synchronously in `START_NORMAL`, and resolve FIFO-created `mWorkoutID` for matching `stoppingBaseFileName` inside `enqueueAsyncFinalization()`.
  2. *`WorkoutPostProcessingService` Generation-Guarded FGS Lifecycle (`REQ-TRK-095.2`)*: `WorkoutPostProcessingService` SHALL synchronize `pendingFgsGeneration`, `promotedFgsGeneration`, `stopRequestedForGeneration`, and `activeInstance` under `lifecycleLock`. `startForPostProcessing()` SHALL coalesce redundant starts while already promoted or pending (clearing any pending stop request), `stopIfRunning()` MUST NOT invoke `appContext.stopService(...)` while `pendingFgsGeneration > 0L` (recording `stopRequestedForGeneration` so `onStartCommand()` promotes via `ServiceCompat.startForeground(...)` first and immediately calls `demoteForeground()` + `performStopSelf()`), and `ACTION_STOP` with `pendingFgsGeneration > 0L` MUST promote before stopping.
  3. *Multi-OS Android 12/15/16 & 9-Locale Sweep Verification (`REQ-TRK-095.3`)*: `MultiLocaleUiSweepAndroidTest` SHALL preserve rapid Start$\rightarrow$Pause$\rightarrow$Stop stress timing while reliably capturing `syntheticWorkoutId` before stop and awaiting post-processing drain; `tools/run_multi_os_emulator_sweep.py` (with `ATT-9510` `/data/local/tmp/att_sweep_dump.xml`) MUST pass `12/12` across Android 12 (`Pixel_5_Android_12`), Android 15 (`Pixel_8_Android_15`), Android 16 (`Pixel_7_Android_36`), and all 9 locales.
  4. *Monolith & SWE.3 Parity Invariants (`REQ-TRK-095.4`)*: `TrackerService.java` MUST remain `<= 3,220` LoC, `TrainingApplication.java` MUST remain `<= 2,050` LoC, `docs/architecture/detailed_design/tracker_service.md` SHALL document the FGS handshake (`REQ-PRO-114`), and `0` UI strings SHALL change (`Localization Parity: N/A`).

**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:
• *Given* `startTracking()` succeeded and `stopTracking()` fires before `TrackerService.onCreate()`, *When* `onStartCommand(START_NORMAL)` runs, *Then* premature `stopService()` is suppressed, `performStartForeground()` executes before `performStopForeground()`/`performStopSelf()`, `0` orphan workout row is created, and rapid `START_NORMAL` $\rightarrow$ `ACTION_STOP` commits `FINISHED = 1` for the FIFO-created `workoutId`.
• *Given* `WorkoutPostProcessingService.startForPostProcessing()` succeeded and `drainQueue()` finishes before `onStartCommand()`, *When* `stopIfRunning()` and `onStartCommand()` execute, *Then* `stopService()` is suppressed while pending, `onStartCommand()` promotes then self-stops cleanly, and a concurrent re-enqueue clears the pending stop.
• *Given* the fixed APK on Android 12, 15, 16 and all 9 locales, *When* `run_multi_os_emulator_sweep.py` runs, *Then* all `12/12` runs `PASS` with `0` `ForegroundServiceDidNotStartInTimeException` crashes.

**Invariants**: `ATT-6178` non-blocking `onDestroy` (`< 100 ms`); `ATT-9154` `shortService` `onTimeout` isolation; `REQ-TRK-015`/`022` FGS permission gating (`0` `connectedDevice`); `TrackerService.java <= 3,220` LoC; `TrainingApplication.java <= 2,050` LoC; `Localization Parity: N/A - no UI strings`.

### REQ-FIL-040: Self-Adapting Intra-Bucket Barometer Dispersion Variance, 3-State Startup Calibration Grace & ERTS Low-Speed Braking Slope Stability (`ATT-9602`) [Verified]
- **Target Release**: `V5.0.0`
- **Tests**: `TST-FIL-040`
- **System Requirement(s)**: `SYSREQ-TRK-008` — Refines live barometric pressure & EKF altitude/slope fusion (`REQ-FIL-026`, `REQ-FIL-022.3`, `REQ-FIL-033.1`, `REQ-FIL-037.2`).
- **Priority**: High
- **Type**: Functional
- **Verification Method**: Unit
- **Implementation**: `BarometricBucketDispersion.kt`, `AltitudeAndSlopeDevice_ATTFused.kt`, `KinematicAltitudeSlopeEkf.kt`, `ExtendedRtsAltitudeSlopeSmoother.kt`
- **Localization Parity**: `N/A - no UI strings`
- **Rationale**: Real-world Pixel 10 pocket-cycling telemetry (`2026-11-22_081402.phone_raw.csv.gz`) exposed three altitude-fusion defects: (1) asymmetric `~0.92 Hz` pocket-compression pressure waves (`70–88 m` peak-to-peak) beat against fixed 1-Hz baro buckets (`+6..+12 m` monotonic median bias invisible to sign-reversal jitter detection), inflating live `att_fused_altitude_slope` by `+6.74 m` / `+10%` on flat ground and doubling ascent/descent (`33.6 m` vs `18.8 m` ERTS), while also tripping `computeStepGatedRefVariance()` to veto true flat DEM; (2) uncalibrated `1013.25 hPa` standard-atmosphere altitude is emitted on `rows 0..1` before DEM/GPS lock at `row 2` (`470.9 -> 435.0 m` cliff); and (3) ERTS `SLOPE` plunges to `-11%` during flat-ground end-of-ride braking (`5.5 -> 0.6 m/s`) when pocket decompression pulls baro `-2.07 m` below flat DEM (`ATT-9602`).
- **Description**:
  1. *Self-Adapting Intra-Bucket Barometer Dispersion & Multi-Second Anti-Aliasing (`REQ-FIL-040.1`)*:
     - Pure-Kotlin `BarometricBucketDispersion` SHALL measure intra-bucket physical dispersion (`IQR = Q3 - Q1` for `size >= 4`, `0.5 * (max - min)` for `size in 2..3`, `0.0` for `size < 2`) against a speed-aware physical elevation allowance `physicalSpan = max(2.5, 0.20 * max(0.0, speedMps))`.
     - When `excessSpread = max(0.0, spread - physicalSpan) == 0.0` (handlebar mounts, calm pockets, and real `8–12%` climbs or fast alpine descents with `span <= 2 m`), `dispersionVarM2` SHALL remain `0.0` and the exact 1-Hz bucket median SHALL be emitted with zero added lag (passing speed-coupled slew `speedMps = speed` to `baroFilter.filterSlewOnly()`).
     - When `excessSpread > 0.0` (severe pocket compression / wind buffeting), `BarometricBucketDispersion` SHALL inflate `dispersionVarM2` immediately (`min(2500.0, 2.0 * excessSpread^2)`) with exponential decay (`0.65` retention) on recovery, emit the interquartile trimmed mean across a bounded rolling `3,000 ms` raw sample window (`<= 128` samples, spanning `>= 2–3` pedal cycles), keep `baroFilter.filterSlewOnly()` at tight base slew (`speedMps = 0.0`), and feed `varBaro = mEffectiveRBaro + dispersionVarM2` into `KinematicAltitudeSlopeEkf.updateBarometer()`.
  2. *Corrupted-Barometer DEM Step-Gate Protection (`REQ-FIL-040.2`)*:
     - `KinematicAltitudeSlopeEkf` SHALL track `lastBaroVarM2` (updated across all `updateBarometer()` paths using raw finite positive `varBaro`, reset in `reset()`/`reanchorOnResume()`/`setMode()`) and apply baro-vs-ref step-discrepancy gating in `computeStepGatedRefVariance()` and DEM-to-slope decoupling (`k1Eff = 0`) ONLY when `lastBaroVarM2 <= DEM_BARO_TRUST_VAR_THRESHOLD` (`16.0 m²`), clearing `lastRefBaroAltM = NaN` while untrusted so a dispersion-corrupted pocket barometer neither vetoes flat DEM/GPS anchoring nor locks a stale slope bias while preserving 100% of `REQ-FIL-037` whenever the barometer is healthy (`varBaro <= 16.0 m²`).
  3. *3-State Startup Calibration Grace Guard (`REQ-FIL-040.3`)*:
     - `AltitudeAndSlopeDevice_ATTFused` SHALL record `mFirstBaroTimestampMs` on initial barometer ingestion (cleared in `shutDown()`) and defer publishing `mAltitudeSensor` / `mSlopeSensor` / `mVerticalSpeedSensor` / `mAscentSensor` / `mDescentSensor` for up to `STARTUP_REFERENCE_GRACE_MS = 5_000L` strictly when `ekf.is3StateMode && !ekf.hasReferenceAnchor` (`ekf.isInitialized` MUST still become `true` immediately on sample #1).
     - Upon arrival of a DEM/GPS reference within the grace window, calibrated altitude SHALL be emitted immediately with zero startup cliff and `0.0 m` phantom ascent/descent; if no reference arrives within `5 s` (offline/indoor), publication SHALL fall back seamlessly to barometer altitude (preserving `REQ-FIL-022.3` zero bogus descent on later DEM lock), while 2-State mode (`!is3StateMode`) and `reanchorOnResume()` MUST continue publishing immediately (`0 ms` delay).
  4. *ERTS Low-Speed Braking Slope Stability (`REQ-FIL-040.4`)*:
     - `ExtendedRtsAltitudeSlopeSmoother` SHALL preserve `LOW_SPEED_SLOPE_THRESHOLD_MPS = 1.2` and `lowSpeedSlopeGainFactor()` unchanged (`TST-FIL-033.1`), while detecting active deceleration (`dv/dt <= -0.25 m/s²` with a `4.0 s` low-speed braking hold below `2.5 m/s`): during braking, inflate `effectiveVarBaro` (`8.0×` with DEM/GPS reference, `4.0×` without reference below `2.5 m/s`) and smoothly damp low-speed braking slope updates & curvature (`qgVal` & baro `k1` scaled by `(speedMps / 2.5)^2` below `2.5 m/s`) so flat-ground braking (`5.5 -> 0.6 m/s`) avoids `-11%` grade plunges while steady-speed climbs/descents and genuine downhill braking remain accurate.

**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:
• *Given* raw `~13 Hz` barometer telemetry with a `70–88 m` asymmetric `~0.92 Hz` pocket-compression wave on a flat `436–438 m` road (`t = 25..65 s`, `DEM = 436..438 m`, `speed = 5.5 m/s`, `QNH +34 m`) and a full-workout loop with pocket waves + genuine climbs/descents, *When* `AltitudeAndSlopeDevice_ATTFused` processes the stream, *Then* live fused `ALTITUDE` stays within `<= 2.0 m` of true elevation (no `+6.7 m` / `+10%` phantom climb) and full-workout live `ASCENT`/`DESCENT` stay within `<= 20%` of ERTS / true gain.
• *Given* a cyclist climbing or descending a real `8–12%` alpine grade on a handlebar mount or calm pocket (`intra-bucket span <= 2 m`), *When* `AltitudeAndSlopeDevice_ATTFused` processes the stream, *Then* `dispersionVarM2` remains `0.0` (`varBaro` uninflated) and live altitude & slope % track the true grade promptly with zero added lag.
• *Given* a 3-State workout starting outdoors with `+35 m` QNH offset and DEM/GPS arriving at `t = 1–2 s`, *When* the first `5 s` of samples are recorded, *Then* `ALTITUDE` does not emit a `470.9 -> 435.0 m` (`-35.7 m`) cliff or inject phantom ascent/descent; *When* completely offline for `>= 5 s`, *Then* it falls back gracefully to baro altitude; *When* in 2-State mode, *Then* it publishes immediately on sample #1.
• *Given* a flat-road `15 s` braking segment (`5.5 -> 0.6 m/s`, `DEM = 438 m`, `BARO` dropping `-2.0 m` on pocket decompression), *When* `ExtendedRtsAltitudeSlopeSmoother.smooth()` runs, *Then* smoothed `SLOPE` stays within realistic flat-road bounds (`|SLOPE| <= 4%`, no `-11%` plunge) while steady climbs/descents remain unaffected.

**Invariants**: `DEFAULT_VAR_BARO = 0.25` & 2-state `p00 = varBaro` unchanged; `REQ-FIL-037` DEM jump gating unchanged when `varBaro <= 16.0 m²`; `LOW_SPEED_SLOPE_THRESHOLD_MPS = 1.2` & `lowSpeedSlopeGainFactor()` unchanged (`TST-FIL-033.1`); `ekf.isInitialized == true` on sample #1; `REQ-FIL-022.3` `0.0 m` bogus descent on late DEM lock preserved; all modified files `<= 1,500` LoC (`0` protected monolith edits); `0` SQLite/UI/`strings*.xml` changes (`Localization Parity: N/A`).

### REQ-FIL-041: Immediate Zero-Speed Publication on Stationary Rejected GNSS Fixes & Post-Pause Velocity Spike Bounding (`ATT-9601`) [Verified]
- **Target Release**: `V5.0.0`
- **Tests**: `TST-FIL-041`
- **System Requirement(s)**: `SYSREQ-TRK-010` — Refines smartphone fused odometry speed publication and post-pause position-velocity decoupling (`REQ-FIL-023.1`, `REQ-FIL-036.2`, `REQ-TRK-056.2`).
- **Priority**: High
- **Type**: Functional
- **Verification Method**: Unit
- **Implementation**: `SpeedAndLocationDevice.java`, `UnicycleOdometryEkf.kt`, `SpeedAndLocationDevice_ATTFused.kt`
- **Localization Parity**: `N/A - no UI strings`
- **Rationale**: Real-world indoor-stop cycling telemetry (`2026-11-22_081402`) revealed two defects in `SpeedAndLocationDevice_ATTFused`: (1) when entering an indoor stop with coarse GNSS accuracy (`acc = 45.9 m > MAX_GNSS_ACCURACY_METERS`), `onLocationChanged()` rejected the fix and returned early without publishing `0.0 m/s` to `mSpeedSensor` / `null` to `mPaceSensor`, freezing `1.536 m/s` for `14 s` until the `10 s` watchdog decayed it; and (2) upon exiting after a `159 s` stop (`row 228 -> row 229`, `+19.1 m` coordinate snap, Doppler `3.51 m/s`), both un-decoupled EKF post-pause `P[pos, v]` cross-covariance and `SpeedAndLocationDevice.java`'s `rawSpeed < 0.2 * calcSpeed` geometric override (`(2.61 + 19.09)/2 = 10.85 m/s`) spiked live speed to `39.1 km/h` (`ATT-9601`).
- **Description**:
  1. *Immediate Stationary Zero-Speed Publication (`REQ-FIL-041.1`)*:
     - `SpeedAndLocationDevice` SHALL expose `protected synchronized void publishStationaryZeroSpeed()` cancelling pending watchdog decay, setting `mIsDecaying = false` and `mSpeed = 0.0`, publishing `0.0` to `mSpeedSensor` and `null` to `mPaceSensor`, while leaving pinned coordinates, `mStationaryAnchor`, `mPrevLocation`, and distance accumulators strictly unchanged (`+0.0 m`).
     - `SpeedAndLocationDevice_ATTFused.onLocationChanged()` SHALL invoke `publishStationaryZeroSpeed()` strictly outside `synchronized(ekf)` (preserving `this -> ekf` lock ordering) before returning early when `!gnssAccepted && (isStationary || fusedSpeed <= SPEED_ZERO_THRESHOLD)` (including pre-anchor coarse stationary fixes).
  2. *Post-Pause & Post-Standstill EKF Position-Velocity Decoupling (`REQ-FIL-041.2`)*:
     - `UnicycleOdometryEkf` SHALL track `lastAcceptedMovingGnssTimestampMs` and `postPauseDecoupleRemainingFixes` (cleared in `resetGnssTrackingState()`). Executing `applyZeroVelocityUpdate()`, receiving a stationary GNSS fix (`isStationary == true`), or observing a `>= POST_PAUSE_DECOUPLE_GAP_MS` (`5_000L` ms) GNSS or moving-GNSS gap SHALL arm `postPauseDecoupleRemainingFixes = POST_PAUSE_DECOUPLE_FIXES` (`3`).
     - While `postPauseDecoupleRemainingFixes > 0` on moving GNSS updates (`!isStationary`), `updateGnssPosition()` SHALL decouple position-to-velocity gain (`k[2][0] = k[2][1] = 0.0`) and zero velocity cross-covariances (`decoupleVelocityCovariance(pNew)`), decrementing strictly on accepted moving inliers (`consecutiveMovingOutliers == 0`) without throttling `updateSpeed()` (Doppler or wheel).
  3. *Fused-Speed Trust Hook & `REQ-TRK-056.2` Preservation (`REQ-FIL-041.3`)*:
     - `SpeedAndLocationDevice` SHALL expose `protected boolean trustsFusedLocationSpeed() { return false; }`, keeping `REQ-TRK-056.2` (`rawSpeed < 0.2 * calcSpeed` geometric fallback) 100% unchanged for `SpeedAndLocationDevice_GPS` and `SpeedAndLocationDevice_GoogleFused`. `SpeedAndLocationDevice_ATTFused` SHALL override `trustsFusedLocationSpeed()` to return `true` so `onNewLocation()` preserves EKF-fused positive `rawSpeed` across post-pause coordinate snaps while keeping `SpeedAndLocationDevice_ATTFused.kt <= 1,500` LoC.

**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:
• *Given* `SpeedAndLocationDevice_ATTFused` moving at `1.54 m/s` entering an indoor stop (`acc = 45.9 m > 20 m`, `PhoneCarriageMode.STATIONARY`), *When* `onLocationChanged()` rejects the coarse fix with `isStationary == true`, *Then* `mSpeedSensor` immediately publishes `0.0 m/s` and `mPaceSensor` `null` on that tick while pinned coordinates and cumulative distance remain unchanged (`+0.0 m`).
• *Given* a workout resuming after a `159 s` indoor stop with a `+19.1 m` outdoor snap (`acc = 11.73 m`, Doppler `3.51 m/s`), *When* `SpeedAndLocationDevice_ATTFused` processes the first post-pause fixes, *Then* `SPEED_mps_att_fused` stays within `<= 1.5 m/s` of `3.51 m/s` and does NOT spike to `10.85 m/s`.
• *Given* a cyclist accelerating legitimately from standstill to `9.0 m/s` with valid outdoor GNSS (`acc <= 10 m`, Doppler `9.0 m/s`) or active wheel sensor, *When* `SpeedAndLocationDevice_ATTFused` processes the start, *Then* `SPEED_mps_att_fused` tracks `9.0 m/s` promptly without clamping or added lag.

**Invariants**: `SpeedAndLocationDevice_ATTFused.kt <= 1,500` LoC; strict `this -> ekf` lock order; `REQ-TRK-056.2` untouched for `GPS` & `GoogleFused`; `MIN_DOPPLER_SPEED_MPS = 3.0`, `ZUPT_SPEED_VARIANCE = 2.0`, `MAX_GNSS_ACCURACY_METERS = 20.0`, `coldStartHelper`, and `ExtendedRtsOdometrySmoother` preserved; `0` DB/UI/`strings*.xml` changes (`Localization Parity: N/A`).

### REQ-FIL-042: Unified Monotonic `elapsedRealtime` Clock Domain Across GNSS, IMU & Wheel Streams in `SpeedAndLocationDevice_ATTFused` [Verified]
- **Target Release**: `V5.0.0`
- **Tests**: `TST-FIL-042`
- **System Requirement(s)**: `SYSREQ-TRK-010` — Refines smartphone IMU kinematics, carriage classification & fused odometry clock-domain coherence (`REQ-FIL-028.1`/`.3`, `REQ-FIL-029.3`, `REQ-FIL-031.1`).
- **Priority**: High
- **Type**: Functional
- **Verification Method**: Unit
- **Implementation**: `AttFusedTimestampNormalizer.kt`, `SpeedAndLocationDevice_ATTFused.kt`, `PhoneCarriageClassifier.kt`, `UnicycleOdometryEkf.kt`
- **Localization Parity**: `N/A - no UI strings`
- **Rationale**: Sidecar telemetry from Pixel 10 workout `2026-11-22_081402` (`Zum Bäcker #60`, `workoutID = 2428`) revealed a `~2.18 s` offset between GPS `Location.getTime()` and `System.currentTimeMillis()` at matching `CLOCK_BOOTTIME` (`elapsedRealtimeNs`). Passing `location.time` into `PhoneCarriageClassifier` and `UnicycleOdometryEkf` while IMU/baro/step/wheel callbacks used wall-clock `System.currentTimeMillis()` caused a `-2.18 s` backward timestamp sawtooth on every 1-Hz fix and bypassed `dismountHysteresisMs` (`2.0 s`), triggering sub-second `4 <-> 1` (`BIKE_FRONT_POCKET <-> WALKING_TRANSITION`) chatter (`ATT-9600`).
- **Description**:
  1. *Unified `CLOCK_BOOTTIME` (`elapsedRealtime`) Internal Domain & UTC External Boundaries (`REQ-FIL-042.1`)*:
     - `SpeedAndLocationDevice_ATTFused` SHALL unify all internal telemetry consumers (`UnicycleOdometryEkf`, `PhoneCarriageClassifier`, `KinematicSportTypeDiscriminator`, `AttFusedColdStartHelper`, `SmartphoneImuCadenceEstimator`, chord/displacement buffers, and wheel freshness checks) onto Android's monotonic `elapsedRealtime` millisecond clock via `AttFusedTimestampNormalizer`.
     - GNSS `Location` timestamps SHALL derive from `location.elapsedRealtimeNanos / 1_000_000L` when `> 0L` (guarding against `> 300 s` / epoch-ns broken HALs), falling back to platform `elapsedRealtime` for Android wall-clock fixes with `elapsedRealtimeNanos <= 0L`, while preserving synthetic `< 1.5e12` / plain-JVM `location.time` test inputs. External UTC boundaries (`MultisportTelemetryAnchor.timestampMs` via NTP-immune `toWallClockEpochMs()`, `SmartphoneRawTelemetryRecorder` `epochMs`) MUST remain UTC wall-clock.
  2. *Cross-Device OEM `SensorEvent` & Wheel Timestamp Normalization (`REQ-FIL-042.2`)*:
     - `AttFusedTimestampNormalizer` SHALL map standard `CLOCK_BOOTTIME` `SensorEvent`s (`|sensorMs - elapsedMs| <= 5_000 ms`) directly (`offset = 0`, preserving CHRE/FIFO `dt`), latch and drift-reanchor per-sensor-type offsets for divergent OEM HALs (`uptimeMillis` after deep sleep or Unix epoch ns with `> 5_000 ms` offset) without freezing under paused Robolectric clocks, normalize wall-clock wheel callbacks (`>= 1.5e12`) onto `elapsedRealtime`, and degrade cleanly to `currentTimeMillis()` on plain JVM when `SystemClock` is unmocked.
  3. *Defensive Out-of-Order Classifier/EKF Monotonic Guards & Monolith Cap (`REQ-FIL-042.3`)*:
     - `PhoneCarriageClassifier.classify(nowMs)` SHALL clamp `effectiveNowMs = max(nowMs, lastEventTimestampMs)` so out-of-order/lagged GNSS or CHRE timestamps NEVER seed `candidateSinceMs` in the past or bypass dwell hysteresis, and `ingestGnssSpeed()` SHALL guard against older out-of-order timestamps. `UnicycleOdometryEkf` SHALL keep `currentTimestampMs` monotonically non-decreasing, and `SpeedAndLocationDevice_ATTFused.kt` MUST stay strictly `<= 1,500` LoC.

**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:
• *Given* GPS `Location.getTime()` lags `System.currentTimeMillis()` by `2.18 s` while `Location.getElapsedRealtimeNanos()` matches `SystemClock.elapsedRealtimeNanos()` within `< 200 ms`, *When* `SpeedAndLocationDevice_ATTFused` processes interleaved 50-Hz IMU and 1-Hz GNSS callbacks, *Then* `PhoneCarriageClassifier` enforces the full `dismountHysteresisMs` (`2.0 s`) / `mountHysteresisMs` (`4.0 s`) dwell windows with zero sub-`2 s` `4 <-> 1` flips and zero `-2.18 s` timestamp sawtooths.
• *Given* an Android device whose `SensorEvent.timestamp` uses `uptimeMillis` or epoch nanoseconds (`> 5 s` offset from `SystemClock.elapsedRealtimeNanos()`), *When* IMU, wheel, and GNSS events arrive concurrently, *Then* `AttFusedTimestampNormalizer` maps all streams cleanly onto `SystemClock.elapsedRealtime()` without breaking `dt` or sliding windows.
• *Given* a unit/replay test reproducing the `t = 4..20 s` window from `2026-11-22_081402.phone_raw.csv.gz` (`-2.18 s` GNSS offset) and out-of-order `PhoneCarriageClassifier.classify()` calls, *When* the test suite runs, *Then* zero sub-`2 s` mode-chatter flips occur and all existing `SpeedAndLocationDevice_ATTFused`, `PhoneCarriageClassifier`, `UnicycleOdometryEkf`, and replay tests pass.

**Invariants**: `SpeedAndLocationDevice_ATTFused.kt <= 1,500` LoC; leaf-only normalizer lock (zero lock inversion); `TST-FIL-029.3` CHRE step batch spacing preserved; UTC `MultisportTelemetryAnchor.timestampMs` & sidecar `epochMs` preserved; `SpeedAndLocationDevice.java` & `MySensorManager.java` untouched; `0` DB/UI/`strings*.xml` edits (`Localization Parity: N/A`).

### REQ-CON-056: Radar Subsystem & State-Cache Consolidation, 3-StateFlow Repository Split & AB-BA Deadlock Elimination (`ATT-8132`) [Verified]
- **Target Release**: `V5.0.0`
- **Tests**: `TST-CON-056`
- **System Requirement(s)**: `SYSREQ-TRK-006`, `SYSREQ-TRK-007`
- **Priority**: High
- **Type**: Functional
- **Verification Method**: Unit, Integration
- **Implementation**: `TuningPreferencesDataStore.kt`, `SettingsDataStore.kt`, `TrainingApplication.java`, `TrackerService.java`, `RadarState.kt`, `RadarAudioSnoozeState.kt`, `RadarStateRepository.kt`, `RadarSnoozeController.kt`, `RadarLightController.kt`, `SmartBrakeLightDetector.kt`, `SmartRadarLightEngine.kt`, `RadarAlertManager.kt`, `BTLERadarDevice.java`, `ANTBikeRadarDevice.kt`, `ANTBikeLightDevice.kt`, `RadarTrafficSimulator.kt`, `TrackingTabsScreen.kt`, `RadarEdgeOverlay.kt`, `RadarSettingsSection.kt`
- **Localization Parity**: `N/A - no UI strings`
- **Rationale**: Stacked radar/tuning increments left four structural defects (`ATT-8132`, Epic `ATT-8120`): (1) 5 overlapping radar-light DataStore keys producing contradictory fresh-install defaults (`resolveRadarLightAutoMode == true` vs `resolveRadarLightControlMode == FIXED_WORKOUT_MODE`) and partial updates, plus per-startup `LEGACY_MAP_CAMERA_KEYS` purges and `TrainingApplication.setClusterTol*` mirrors; (2) stale intermediate caches (`RadarLightController.cached*` / dead `updateCachedPreferences` / per-tap `CoroutineScope(Dispatchers.IO)` and `TrackerService.mCachedAutoPauseConfig` missing invalidation in 4 mutators); (3) monolithic `RadarState` combined with a 1-Hz `RadarSnoozeController` background ticker waking `SmartRadarLightEngine` (extending `threatCooldownUntilRealtimeMs`) and `RadarAlertManager` every second; and (4) an AB-BA `synchronized` deadlock cycle between `RadarLightController` and `SmartBrakeLightDetector`.
- **Description**:
  1. *Canonical Radar-Light Preference Model & One-Time Migration (`REQ-CON-056.1`)*: `TuningPreferencesDataStore` SHALL persist radar tail-light preferences strictly under `KEY_RADAR_LIGHT_CONTROL_MODE` (unified default `RadarLightControlMode.SMART_AUTOMATION`) and `KEY_RADAR_LIGHT_DEFAULT_MODE` (default `RadarLightMode.DAY_FLASH`). A one-time `DataMigration<Preferences>` wired into `user_settings` DataStore SHALL migrate legacy keys (`KEY_RADAR_LIGHT_AUTO_MODE`, `KEY_RADAR_AUTO_LIGHT_CONTROL`, `KEY_RADAR_SMART_LIGHT_AUTOMATION`) preserving prior user behavior, remove those legacy keys and `LEGACY_MAP_CAMERA_KEYS`, and migrate legacy SharedPreferences cluster tolerances into DataStore. `TuningConfig` boolean projections (`radarLightAutoMode`, `radarAutoLightControl`, `radarSmartLightAutomation`) SHALL be pure derived properties of `radarLightControlMode`; per-startup map-camera purges and `TrainingApplication.setClusterTol*` dual-write mirrors SHALL be deleted while `TrainingApplication.getClusterTol*` delegates to DataStore when initialized.
  2. *Elimination of Stale Intermediate Caches (`REQ-CON-056.2`)*: `RadarLightController` SHALL delete `cached*` fields, `hasLoadedInitialPreferences`, and dead `updateCachedPreferences` overloads, read live preferences directly from `TuningPreferencesDataStore`'s in-memory `StateFlow` (zero `runBlocking`), and execute `persistSelectionAsync` on a single long-lived controller `CoroutineScope(SupervisorJob() + Dispatchers.IO)`. `TrackerService` SHALL delete `mCachedAutoPauseConfig` / `invalidateAutoPauseCache()` and read directly from `SettingsDataStoreJavaHelper.getAutoPauseConfig(this)` (`StateFlow.value`, zero `runBlocking`) while keeping `TrackerService.java <= 3,220` LoC and `TrainingApplication.java <= 2,050` LoC.
  3. *Three Orthogonal Radar StateFlows & Zero 1-Hz Snooze Wakeups (`REQ-CON-056.3`)*: `RadarStateRepository` SHALL split monolithic `RadarState` into `threatState: StateFlow<RadarThreatState>` (arbitrating `BLE > ANT_PLUS > SIMULATOR`, `CONNECTED > CONNECTING`, `sourceStates <= 4`), `lightState: StateFlow<RadarLightState>`, and `snoozeState: StateFlow<RadarAudioSnoozeState>` carrying monotonic `snoozeUntilElapsedRealtimeMs: Long?`, with atomic `MutableStateFlow.update { ... }`. `RadarSnoozeController` SHALL replace its 1-Hz polling loop with a single one-shot expiry timer. `SmartRadarLightEngine` SHALL observe ONLY `threatState` and `RadarAlertManager` SHALL observe `threatState` and `lightState.map { it.lightMode }.distinctUntilChanged()` (zero `snoozeState` wakeups), while UI/ViewModel layers combine slices on demand (`RadarEdgeOverlay` derives badge minutes locally from `snoozeUntilElapsedRealtimeMs`).
  4. *Deadlock-Free Brake-Light Concurrency (`REQ-CON-056.4`)*: `SmartBrakeLightDetector.isEnabled` and `isAutoBypassed` MUST use lock-free `AtomicBoolean`, and neither `RadarLightController` nor `SmartBrakeLightDetector` SHALL invoke any method on the other class, `RadarLightDeviceClient`, `SmartRadarLightEngine`, or `RadarStateRepository` while holding its own monitor ($E(L_A \to L_B) = E(L_B \to L_A) = \emptyset$).

**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:
• *Given* a fresh install or existing profile with legacy radar-light/map-camera/cluster keys, *When* DataStore initializes and radar or Auto-Pause toggles are changed, *Then* migration runs once preserving prior behavior, all queries agree on one canonical model, and changes take immediate effect in `RadarLightController` and `TrackerService` without manual cache invalidation.
• *Given* an active timed audio snooze running concurrently with 8-Hz vehicle telemetry, *When* time advances and radar frames arrive, *Then* snooze never overwrites vehicle targets, `lightState`/`snoozeState` update atomically via `MutableStateFlow.update`, `SmartRadarLightEngine`/`RadarAlertManager` receive **0** 1-Hz countdown wakeups, and snooze expires cleanly at `snoozeUntilElapsedRealtimeMs`.
• *Given* concurrent mode/device registration mutations in `RadarLightController` and IMU deceleration/stop events in `SmartBrakeLightDetector` across parallel threads, *When* both execute under stress, *Then* zero opposite-order nested `synchronized` locks are ever held (`findDeadlockedThreads() == null`).

**Chesterton's Fence (`REQ-PRO-022`)**:
1. *Original Requirement ID & Target*: Refines `REQ-CON-031.5`, `REQ-CON-034`, `REQ-CON-035`, `REQ-CON-041`, `REQ-CON-046`, `REQ-CON-048`, `REQ-TRK-064`, `REQ-MAP-050`.
2. *Historical Origin & Commit Trace*: `b645651f` (`ATT-7538`), `88ed2667` (`ATT-5104`), `1a6606d5` (`ATT-5121`), `302dbd16` (`ATT-8455`), `3376bb3f` (`ATT-8450`), `908a1431` (`ATT-5119`), `07046807` (`ATT-3581`).
3. *Root Reason for Existing Formulation*: Incremental sprints layered new preference keys, local caches, and monolithic `RadarState` fields without consolidating earlier mechanisms.
4. *Preservation of Core Invariants*: `REQ-CON-041` arbitration (`BLE > ANT_PLUS > SIMULATOR`, `CONNECTED > CONNECTING`), `REQ-PRO-130.1` `sourceStates <= 4`, `REQ-CON-035` StVZO/night anti-glare & RearVue 820 auto-bypass, `REQ-CON-048` offline hydration, `REQ-TRK-064` monotonic snooze & UI badge formatting, `TrainingApplication.java <= 2,050` & `TrackerService.java <= 3,220` LoC, zero `runBlocking`, zero `strings*.xml` edits.
