### REQ-CON-057: Capability-Gated Polar Live-IMU HRM Source Selection & Removal of `BIKE_POWER` from Slope/VAM/Ascent/Descent (`ATT-8991`) [Verified]
- **Target Release**: `V5.0.0`
- **Tests**: `TST-CON-057`
- **System Requirement(s)**: `SYSREQ-TRK-001` — Refines multi-sensor profile capability mapping (`DeviceType.getDeviceTypeList`, `DevicesDatabaseManager.getDeviceIdAndNameLists`, `SensorSourceDialog`).
- **Priority**: High
- **Type**: Functional
- **Verification Method**: Unit, Integration
- **Implementation**: `HrmSensorCapabilityHelper.kt`, `DeviceType.java`, `DevicesDatabaseManager.java`, `SensorSourceDialog.kt`
- **Localization Parity**: `N/A - no UI strings`
- **Rationale**: `PolarOnlineStreamingBridge` (stored as `DeviceType.HRM`, `Protocol.BLUETOOTH_LE`) emits `CADENCE` and `STRIDES` on Polar H10 and Verity Sense/OH1, and `VERTICAL_OSCILLATION` and `GROUND_CONTACT_TIME` exclusively on Polar H10. Previously `DeviceType.getDeviceTypeList()` omitted `DeviceType.HRM` from `CADENCE`/`STRIDES`, listed all `HRM` devices unconditionally for `VERTICAL_OSCILLATION`/`GROUND_CONTACT_TIME` (exposing ordinary ANT+/BLE HRMs and Verity Sense), and erroneously mapped `DeviceType.BIKE_POWER` to `SLOPE`, `VERTICAL_SPEED`, `ASCENT`, and `DESCENT` (`ATT-8991`).
- **Description**:
  1. *`DeviceType.getDeviceTypeList()` Polar HRM & Slope/VAM Mapping (`REQ-CON-057.1`)*: `DeviceType.getDeviceTypeList(SensorType)` SHALL include `DeviceType.HRM` for `SensorType.CADENCE` and `SensorType.STRIDES` (preserving existing cadence/stride device types), retain `DeviceType.HRM` for `SensorType.VERTICAL_OSCILLATION` and `SensorType.GROUND_CONTACT_TIME`, and SHALL remove `DeviceType.BIKE_POWER` from `SensorType.SLOPE`, `SensorType.VERTICAL_SPEED`, `SensorType.ASCENT`, and `SensorType.DESCENT` (retaining `DeviceType.VERTICAL_SPEED_AND_SLOPE` and `DeviceType.ALTITUDE_AND_SLOPE_ATT_FUSED`).
  2. *Hardware-Grounded HRM Capability Helper (`REQ-CON-057.2`)*: `HrmSensorCapabilityHelper.isSensorSupported(context, sensorType, protocol, deviceName, manufacturer, btAddress)` SHALL gate `CADENCE`/`STRIDES` strictly to `Protocol.BLUETOOTH_LE` devices classified by `PolarOfflineGattCoordinator` as Polar H10 or Polar Verity Sense/OH1 (`REQ-CON-049.4`), gate `VERTICAL_OSCILLATION`/`GROUND_CONTACT_TIME` strictly to `Protocol.BLUETOOTH_LE` Polar H10 devices, reject `null` sensor types, ANT+ HRMs (including dual-protocol ANT+ Polar H10), confirmed non-Polar GATT HRMs, and non-Polar/H9 BLE HRMs on those 4 metrics, and return `true` for all HRMs on `HR`, `RR_INTERVAL`, `HRV_RMSSD`, `HRV_DFA_A1`, `HRV_ARTIFACT_RATE`, and `HRV_SDNN` (`REQ-HRV-050.1`).
  3. *`DevicesDatabaseManager` & `SensorSourceDialog` Parity (`REQ-CON-057.3`)*: `DevicesDatabaseManager.getDeviceIdAndNameLists(SensorType)` SHALL filter `DeviceType.HRM` rows via `HrmSensorCapabilityHelper.isSensorSupported(...)` using safe `>= 0` cursor column-index extraction while preserving `BIKE_POWER` flag filtering; `SensorSourceDialog.sortNotConnectedDevices()` SHALL apply the identical `HrmSensorCapabilityHelper.isSensorSupported(...)` filter for `DeviceType.HRM` entries while preserving `REQ-UI-2804.3` HR modality sorting.
  4. *Monolith & Zero-Regression Invariants (`REQ-CON-057.4`)*: Keep `DevicesDatabaseManager.java <= 1,671` LoC (`0` upward `.ui.*` imports), `SensorSourceDialog.kt <= 1,500` LoC, `HrmSensorCapabilityHelper.kt <= 1,500` LoC, `MySensorManager` priority tables unchanged, zero DB schema bumps, and `0` `strings*.xml` edits (`Localization Parity: N/A - no UI strings`).

**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:
• *Given* paired BLE Polar H10 (renamed with hardware `H10` metadata or legacy name), BLE Polar Verity Sense, ANT+ Polar H10, ordinary BLE/ANT+ HRMs, Bike Power meter, and Run Footpod, *When* `DeviceType.getDeviceTypeList()`, `DevicesDatabaseManager.getDeviceIdAndNameLists()`, and `SensorSourceDialog.sortNotConnectedDevices()` execute, *Then* `CADENCE`/`STRIDES` include BLE H10 & Verity Sense and exclude ANT+ H10 & ordinary HRMs; `VERTICAL_OSCILLATION`/`GROUND_CONTACT_TIME` include ONLY BLE H10; `SLOPE`/`VERTICAL_SPEED`/`ASCENT`/`DESCENT` exclude `BIKE_POWER`; and `HR`/`RR_INTERVAL`/`HRV_*` continue to include all HRMs.

**Chesterton's Fence (`REQ-PRO-022`)**:
1. *Original Requirement ID & Target*: Refines `REQ-SET-112` (`ATT-8016`), `REQ-SET-115` (`ATT-8018`), `REQ-CON-049` (`ATT-8717`/`ATT-8949`), `REQ-HRV-050.1` (`ATT-8990`), `REQ-UI-2804.3` (`ATT-9610`).
2. *Historical Origin & Commit Trace*: `ce73751c` (`SCRUM-140`/`144`), `75c1497e` (`ATT-6279`), `6be4eb31` (`ATT-8018`).
3. *Root Reason for Existing Formulation*: `DeviceType.getDeviceTypeList()` lacked per-device HRM capability filtering parallel to `BikePowerSensorsHelper`, and `SCRUM-140` speculatively included `BIKE_POWER` on slope/VAM.
4. *Preservation of Core Invariants*: All HR/RR/HRV HRM source selection (`REQ-HRV-050.1`), `BikePowerSensorsHelper` gating, `MySensorManager` priority tables, `SensorSourceDialog` HR modality/health ordering (`REQ-UI-2804.3`), `EditSensorFieldDialog` `"Best Sensor"` fallback, and monolith/schema limits remain 100% preserved.

### REQ-CON-058: Pre-Dereference Null Guard and Standstill `null` Pace in `ANTBikeSpeedDevice` and `ANTBikeSpeedAndCadenceDevice` (`ATT-9605`) [Verified]
- **Target Release**: `V5.0.0`
- **Tests**: `TST-CON-058`
- **System Requirement(s)**: `SYSREQ-TRK-001` — Refines ANT+ Bike Speed and Speed & Cadence telemetry acquisition safety (`ANTBikeSpeedDevice.java`, `ANTBikeSpeedAndCadenceDevice.java`).
- **Priority**: Medium
- **Type**: Functional
- **Verification Method**: Unit
- **Implementation**: `ANTBikeSpeedDevice.java`, `ANTBikeSpeedAndCadenceDevice.java`
- **Localization Parity**: `N/A - 0 string changes`
- **Rationale**: In `ANTBikeSpeedDevice.onNewCalculatedSpeed` (`:112-117`) and `ANTBikeSpeedAndCadenceDevice.onNewCalculatedSpeed` (`:148-151`), `calculatedSpeed.doubleValue()` was dereferenced unconditionally (including inside `myLog(...)` in `ANTBikeSpeedDevice:113`), risking `NullPointerException` on a `null` ANT+ SDK callback, and `1 / calculatedSpeed.doubleValue()` emitted `Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY` at standstill (`0.0`), defeating `ThresholdSensor` (`SPEED_THRESHOLD = 8`) standstill decay semantics and diverging from `ANTBikePowerDevice` (`ATT-6176`, `REQ-STB-026`) and `BTLEBikeDevice` (`ATT-6042`).
- **Description**:
  1. *Pre-Dereference Null Guard (`REQ-CON-058.1`)*: `ANTBikeSpeedDevice.onNewCalculatedSpeed` and `ANTBikeSpeedAndCadenceDevice.onNewCalculatedSpeed` SHALL check `if (calculatedSpeed == null) return;` prior to any logging (`myLog(...)`) or `calculatedSpeed.doubleValue()` dereference, throwing zero exceptions and leaving `mSpeedSensor` and `mPaceSensor` values undisturbed.
  2. *Direct `m/s` Speed Publication & Standstill `null` Pace (`REQ-CON-058.2`)*: For non-null `calculatedSpeed` (in `m/s` per `AntPlusBikeSpeedDistancePcc.CalculatedSpeedReceiver`), both callbacks SHALL publish `double speed = calculatedSpeed.doubleValue();` directly to `mSpeedSensor.newValue(speed)` (no `/ 3.6` scaling) and SHALL publish `mPaceSensor.newValue(speed > 0.0 ? 1.0 / speed : null)`, emitting finite `1.0 / speed` (`s/m`) when `speed > 0.0` and `null` (never `Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY` or negative pace) when `speed <= 0.0`, preserving `ThresholdSensor` (`SPEED_THRESHOLD = 8`) initial `null` and 8-tick standstill decay semantics.

**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:
• *Given* a subscribed `ANTBikeSpeedDevice` or `ANTBikeSpeedAndCadenceDevice` (initial `null` or holding prior motion readings), *When* `onNewCalculatedSpeed(..., null)` fires, *Then* it returns immediately without NPE (including `myLog` path) and leaves `mSpeedSensor` and `mPaceSensor` untouched.
• *Given* a subscribed `ANTBikeSpeedDevice` or `ANTBikeSpeedAndCadenceDevice`, *When* `BigDecimal.ZERO` (`0.0 m/s`), `BigDecimal("10.0")` (`10.0 m/s`), `8` consecutive `BigDecimal.ZERO` standstill ticks, or negative speed arrive, *Then* initial standstill sets `speed == 0.0` & `pace == null`, `10.0 m/s` sets `speed == 10.0` & `pace == 0.1`, ticks `1..7` hold `0.1` and tick `8` decays `mPaceSensor` to `null` (never `Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY`), and negative speed feeds `null` pace.

**Chesterton's Fence (`REQ-PRO-022`)**:
1. *Original Requirement ID & Target*: Net-new `REQ-CON-058` extending `REQ-CON-001` (*ANT+ Sensor Support*), `REQ-CON-025` (`ANTBikeSpeedAndCadenceDevice`), and aligning with `REQ-STB-026` (`ATT-6176`, `ANTBikePowerDevice`).
2. *Historical Origin & Commit Trace*: Legacy ANT+ bike speed callbacks left `// TODO: correct?` on `1 / calculatedSpeed.doubleValue()`; `ATT-6042` (`db8d50c1`) standardized BLE standstill pace to `null` and `ATT-6176` (`ccc04e9a`) fixed `ANTBikePowerDevice`.
3. *Root Reason for Existing Formulation*: Early ANT+ callback assumed `CalculatedSpeedReceiver` only delivered non-null positive speeds and omitted null/zero guards.
4. *Preservation of Core Invariants*: Eager `addSensors()` initialization, `ThresholdSensor(..., SPEED_THRESHOLD = 8)` smoothing, direct `m/s` scaling, `onNewCalculatedAccumulatedDistance` wheel-rev forwarding, `ANTBikeSpeedAndCadenceDevice.releaseAccess()` (`REQ-CON-025.1`), `<= 1,500` LoC caps, and zero DB/UI/string changes remain 100% preserved.

### REQ-FIL-043: Fast `STATIONARY` Exit on Unambiguous Motion & Continuous Non-Stationary Dwell in `PhoneCarriageClassifier` (`ATT-9469`) [Verified]
- **Target Release**: `V5.0.0`
- **Tests**: `TST-FIL-043`
- **System Requirement(s)**: `SYSREQ-TRK-010` — Refines smartphone carriage classification responsiveness and stationary multipath immunity (`PhoneCarriageClassifier.kt`).
- **Priority**: High
- **Type**: Functional
- **Verification Method**: Unit
- **Implementation**: `PhoneCarriageClassifier.kt`
- **Localization Parity**: `N/A - 0 string changes`
- **Rationale**: Accelerating out of traffic-light or errand stops (`2026-11-21_081228`, Bank stop `rows 406..408`, `5.5–6.1 m/s`) shifted `rawCandidate` across `WALKING_TRANSITION -> BIKE_JERSEY_BACK -> BIKE_FRONT_POCKET`, resetting single-subclass `candidateSinceMs` and trapping `committedMode` in `STATIONARY` for `3–5 s`. Continuous non-stationary dwell plus shortened `<= 1.0 s` fast exit on unambiguous high-confidence motion eliminates multi-second `STATIONARY` tails while preserving indoor multipath GNSS-spike immunity (`REQ-FIL-034.1`, `REQ-FIL-042.3`).
- **Description**:
  1. *Continuous Non-Stationary Exit Dwell & Exit State Sync (`REQ-FIL-043.1`)*: While `committedMode == PhoneCarriageMode.STATIONARY` and `rawCandidate` is a non-stationary active mode (`!= STATIONARY`, `!= UNKNOWN`), `PhoneCarriageClassifier` SHALL accumulate continuous non-stationary dwell via `nonStationarySinceMs` (seeded from monotonic `effectiveNowMs` per `REQ-FIL-042.3`) without resetting the exit timer when `rawCandidate` shifts across non-stationary subclasses (`WALKING_TRANSITION -> BIKE_JERSEY_BACK -> BIKE_FRONT_POCKET`). Returning to `STATIONARY`, encountering an `UNKNOWN` gap (`rawCandidate == UNKNOWN`), triggering a `0 ms` wrist glance, or calling `reset()` MUST clear `nonStationarySinceMs = Long.MIN_VALUE`; committing an exit from `STATIONARY` MUST synchronously set `candidateMode = rawCandidate`, `candidateSinceMs = Long.MIN_VALUE`, and `nonStationarySinceMs = Long.MIN_VALUE`. All non-`STATIONARY` transitions MUST remain unchanged.
  2. *Fast `<= 1.0 s` Exit on Unambiguous Motion (`REQ-FIL-043.2`)*: Define `isAccuracyTrusted(acc: Double?) = acc == null || (acc.isFinite() && acc > 0.0 && acc <= 20.0)` (preserving omitted `null` compatibility while rejecting `Double.NaN`, `±Infinity`, `0.0`, `< 0.0`, and `> 20.0`). While `committedMode == STATIONARY`, when `wheelActiveMoving == true` OR (`effectiveSpeed >= 3.2 m/s` AND `gnssFresh && isAccuracyTrusted(lastGnssAccuracyMeters)` AND `!isPositionStagnant(effectiveNowMs)` AND (`!quietImu` OR `hasConfirmedDisplacement(effectiveNowMs)` requiring `positionSamples.size >= 2`, `(nowMs - last.timestampMs) <= STALENESS_MS`, and `>= 10.0 m` spread across trusted fixes)), `PhoneCarriageClassifier` SHALL apply shortened exit dwell `minOf(dismountHysteresisMs, 1_000L)` and commit directly into `rawCandidate`, operating uniformly without barometer, wheel sensor, or `ACTIVITY_RECOGNITION`.
  3. *Indoor Multipath GNSS-Spike Immunity, Position Buffer Gating & Monotonic Hygiene (`REQ-FIL-043.3`)*: `ingestGnssSpeed()` SHALL persist raw `lastGnssAccuracyMeters = accuracyMeters` under `isNewerOrEqualGnss && speedMps.isFinite() && speedMps >= 0.0` (cleared in `reset()`, never collapsing invalid non-null values to `null`) and SHALL gate `positionSamples.addLast(...)` on `isAccuracyTrusted(accuracyMeters)` so coarse/invalid coordinate jumps never poison `isPositionStagnant(nowMs)`. When `!wheelActiveMoving && (hasOrientation || hasGyro) && quietImu` and `poorGnssAccuracy = gnssFresh && !isAccuracyTrusted(lastGnssAccuracyMeters)`, `evaluateRawCandidate()` SHALL classify `STATIONARY`, ensuring transient `1–2 s` coarse (`> 20 m`) or invalid (`0.0`, `< 0.0`, `NaN`, `Infinity`) indoor GNSS spikes produce zero false exits. `PhoneCarriageClassifier.kt` MUST stay `<= 1,500` LoC with unchanged public JVM signatures.

**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:
• *Given* `PhoneCarriageClassifier` is in `STATIONARY` and the athlete accelerates (`2.42 -> 3.88 -> 4.60 m/s` / `3.5 -> 5.5 -> 6.1 m/s`, `acc <= 20 m`, non-quiet IMU, real displacement `> 10 m`), *When* `rawCandidate` shifts across `WALKING_TRANSITION -> BIKE_JERSEY_BACK -> BIKE_FRONT_POCKET`, *Then* `committedMode` exits `STATIONARY` within `<= 1 s` of crossing `3.2 m/s` and never stays `STATIONARY` for `3 s` at `> 5 m/s`.
• *Given* `PhoneCarriageClassifier` is in `STATIONARY` indoors or at a stop with `quietImu == true`, *When* a transient `1–2 s` coarse (`acc > 20 m`), invalid-accuracy (`NaN`, `Infinity`, `0.0`, `< 0.0`), or stagnant-position GNSS speed spike (`v = 5–7 m/s`, with or without coordinate jump) arrives, *Then* coarse/invalid coordinates are excluded from `positionSamples` (`isPositionStagnant` stays unpoisoned) and `committedMode` remains `STATIONARY` with zero false exits.
• *Given* `PhoneCarriageClassifier` is in `STATIONARY` and sees a brief non-stationary blip followed by an `UNKNOWN` gap (or return to `STATIONARY`), *When* non-stationary motion later resumes, *Then* `nonStationarySinceMs` restarts fresh from `effectiveNowMs` with zero stale accumulated dwell.
• *Given* a barometer-free, wheel-free, `ACTIVITY_RECOGNITION`-denied device or a rigid handlebar mount (`quietImu == true` with `>= 10 m` fresh trusted displacement), *When* accelerating smoothly out of `STATIONARY`, *Then* fast exit and multipath rejection execute deterministically with zero regressions to existing hysteresis tests.

**Chesterton's Fence (`REQ-PRO-022`)**:
1. *Original Requirement ID & Target*: Net-new `REQ-FIL-043` refining `REQ-FIL-028.1` (`ATT-8890`), `REQ-FIL-034.1` (`ATT-9062`), `REQ-FIL-035.3` (`ATT-9157`), and `REQ-FIL-042.3` (`ATT-9600`) in `PhoneCarriageClassifier.kt`.
2. *Historical Origin & Commit Trace*: `ATT-8890` set uniform identical-candidate `2.0 s` dwell; `ATT-9062` added `quietImu` + `isPositionStagnant`; `ATT-9600` added `effectiveNowMs` monotonic clamping.
3. *Root Reason for Existing Formulation*: Prevented transient indoor multipath spikes and brief standstill fidgets from breaking `STATIONARY`.
4. *Preservation of Core Invariants*: Retains `1.0 s` minimum dwell on fast exit (blocking single-sample spikes), adds `acc > 20 m` indoor `quietImu` stationary guard, preserves all non-`STATIONARY` dwell timings and `REQ-FIL-042.3` monotonic clamping, and keeps `PhoneCarriageClassifier.kt <= 1,500` LoC with `0` edits to `SpeedAndLocationDevice_ATTFused.kt`, UI, or strings.

### REQ-FIL-044: False `RUN_HANDHELD` Suppression & Kinematic/Footstrike Multisport `RUN` Confirmation Guardrails (`ATT-9467`) [Verified]
- **Target Release**: `V5.0.0`
- **Tests**: `TST-FIL-044`
- **System Requirement(s)**: `SYSREQ-TRK-003`, `SYSREQ-TRK-006`, `SYSREQ-TRK-010` — Refines smartphone carriage classification and multisport `BIKE <-> RUN` transition gating (`PhoneCarriageClassifier.kt`, `MultisportTransitionDetector.kt`, `MultisportTransitionBridge.kt`, `SpeedAndLocationDevice_ATTFused.kt`).
- **Priority**: High
- **Type**: Functional
- **Verification Method**: Unit
- **Implementation**: `PhoneCarriageClassifier.kt`, `MultisportTransitionDetector.kt`, `MultisportTransitionBridge.kt`, `SpeedAndLocationDevice_ATTFused.kt`
- **Localization Parity**: `N/A - 0 string changes`
- **Rationale**: During real-world Pixel 10 pocket-cycling workouts with stops (`2026-11-21_081228`, `workoutID = 2423`), unconstrained `scoreRunHandheld()` (`+10.4` incl. `+3.8` `glanceCount >= 1` held for `15 s`), the `(0.15, 0.35) m/s` / non-pendulum low-speed fallthrough, and time-only `MultisportTransitionDetector` `3 s` / `15 s` windows misclassified bike-pushing (`rows 3..15`, `0.32..0.62 m/s`, `10.9 m`) and a zero-stride `47.8 m` roll (`rows 315..333`, `2.0..4.0 m/s`, `0–1` strides) as `RUN_HANDHELD (7)`, splitting an ordinary cycling ride into 6 phantom triathlon laps (`ATT-9467`).
- **Description**:
  1. *Sub-Running Speed Floor & Footstrike-Gated `RUN_*` / `scoreRunHandheld` (`REQ-FIL-044.1`)*: Define `RUN_MIN_SPEED_MPS = 1.60` and `hasActiveRunningCadence = effectiveRunStepFreqHz != null && effectiveRunStepFreqHz >= ACTIVE_FOOTSTRIKE_MIN_HZ (2.25)`. In `PhoneCarriageClassifier.evaluateRawCandidate()`: (a) when `!wheelActiveMoving && !easyRunSuppressWalk && effectiveSpeed != null && effectiveSpeed < RUN_MIN_SPEED_MPS && !hasActiveRunningCadence`, non-stationary sub-running motion (`(0.10, 1.60) m/s`, incl. irregular bike-pushing/phone handling) SHALL resolve to `PhoneCarriageMode.WALKING_TRANSITION` (never `RUN_*`); (b) when `suppressRunningForNoFootstrikes = !suppressCycling && confirmedNoFootstrikes && sportHint != SportContextHint.RUNNING` holds, all `RUN_*` candidate scores, stale `RUN_*` `committedScore`, and zero-IMU `RUN_WAIST_HIP` fallback MUST be masked (`Double.NEGATIVE_INFINITY` / `BIKE_HANDLEBAR`) while keeping `Sport = RUN` (`TST-FIL-035.1`) and high-impact trail batching gaps (`TST-FIL-039.1`) intact; (c) `scoreRunHandheld()` SHALL award the `glanceCount >= 1` (`+3.8`) bonus strictly when `allowHandheldGlanceBonus = hasActiveRunningCadence || sportHint == SportContextHint.RUNNING || committedMode.isRunningMode()` (otherwise `-1.8`), working uniformly with or without barometer or `ACTIVITY_RECOGNITION`.
  2. *Underflow-Safe Active Footstrike Query & Live Multisport Wiring (`REQ-FIL-044.2`)*: `PhoneCarriageClassifier` SHALL expose `@Synchronized fun hasActiveRunFootstrike(nowMs: Long = lastEventTimestampMs): Boolean` guarding `if (nowMs == Long.MIN_VALUE || firstSampleTimestampMs == Long.MIN_VALUE) return false` BEFORE monotonic `effectiveNowMs` / `pruneWindows()` (preventing signed `Long.MIN_VALUE` underflow) and returning `true` strictly when periodic running footstrikes (`>= 2.25 Hz`) are active via hardware `TYPE_STEP_DETECTOR` OR permission-free 50-Hz accelerometer ACF periodicity. `MultisportTransitionBridge.evaluateAndDispatch()` SHALL preserve `@JvmStatic @JvmOverloads` adding `hasActiveRunFootstrike: Boolean? = null` (retaining both 5-arg and 6-arg Java static bytecode signatures) forwarded to `MultisportTransitionDetector.onTick()`, wired from `SpeedAndLocationDevice_ATTFused.evaluateMultisportTransitionIfDue()` passing monotonic `timestampMs` (not UTC epoch ms) into `carriageClassifier.hasActiveRunFootstrike(timestampMs)` while keeping all touched files `<= 1,500` LoC.
  3. *Bounded Trailing Active-Unpaused Sliding-Window `RUN` Guardrails (`REQ-FIL-044.3`)*: `MultisportTransitionDetector.onTick()` SHALL add `@JvmOverloads hasActiveRunFootstrike: Boolean? = null` and maintain bounded trailing active-unpaused sliding windows (`ArrayDeque<CandidateWindowSample>` recording `activeMs`, `totalDistanceM`, `cumulativeCorroboratedMs`, and `anchor`, pruned to `establishWindowMs` in Phase 1 and `confirmationWindowMs` in Phase 2; cleared in `clearInitialCandidate()`, `clearTargetCandidateOnly()`, and `reset()`), enforcing `MIN_RUN_SPEED_MPS = 1.60` (bypassed when `hardwareSignals.hasActiveRunHardware == true`) with zero-duration guard `windowActiveSec > 0.0` before `windowDistM / windowActiveSec`: (a) Phase 1 `establishedSport = RUN` at `windowActiveMs >= establishWindowMs` SHALL require finite `windowDistM >= 1.60 * (establishWindowMs / 1000.0)` (`4.8 m` at `3 s`), `windowAvgSpeedMps >= 1.60`, AND trailing `windowCorroboratedMs >= minOf(establishWindowMs, (establishWindowMs * 2L) / 3L)` (`2_000 ms` at `3 s`); (b) Phase 2 `BIKE -> RUN` at `windowActiveMs >= confirmationWindowMs` SHALL require finite `windowDistM >= 1.60 * (confirmationWindowMs / 1000.0)` (`24.0 m` at `15 s`), pause-immune `windowAvgSpeedMps >= 1.60`, AND trailing `windowCorroboratedMs >= minOf(confirmationWindowMs, (confirmationWindowMs * 3L) / 5L)` (`9_000 ms` at `15 s`), anchoring `enterAnchor` to the qualifying window head `anchor` while keeping `exitAnchor` intact—eliminating both Phase 2 slow-start lock-out and Phase 1/2 corroboration-dilution (`60 s` uncorroborated roll + brief `5 s` noise burst), while preserving genuine `T1`/`T2` slow joggers (`>= 2.0 m/s`, `~150–160 spm`), parked bike sensors, and phone-only duathlons.

**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:
• *Given* a cycling workout starts with phone handling (`glanceCount >= 1`), a brief `acc > 15 m` GNSS spike, and `0.3–0.8 m/s` bike-pushing with `0` footstrikes (`2026-11-21_081228` `rows 1..25`), *When* `PhoneCarriageClassifier` and `MultisportTransitionDetector` evaluate the first `25 s`, *Then* `RUN_HANDHELD` is never emitted, low-speed pushing resolves to `WALKING_TRANSITION`, and `MultisportTransitionDetector` neither locks `RUN` nor emits a `Laufen -> Rad` lap split.
• *Given* an established `BIKE` session resumes from a stop and rolls `~50 m` at `2.0–4.0 m/s` for `15–20 s` while handling/pocketing the phone with `confirmedNoFootstrikes == true` (`rows 310..340`, with or without barometer), *When* processed, *Then* `RUN_HANDHELD` is suppressed and `0` phantom `BIKE -> TRANSITION -> RUN` transitions emit.
• *Given* a cyclist dismounts at `T2` and transitions into sustained jogging/running (`>= 2.0 m/s`, `>= 15 s`, `~150–160 spm`, with hardware steps OR 50-Hz accel footstrikes, with or without barometer, incl. handheld glance), *When* processed, *Then* `BIKE -> TRANSITION -> RUN` is still detected and anchored accurately.

**Chesterton's Fence (`REQ-PRO-022`)**:
1. *Original Requirement ID & Target*: Net-new `REQ-FIL-044` refining `REQ-FIL-028.1`, `REQ-FIL-031.1`, `REQ-FIL-034.4`, `REQ-FIL-035.1`, `REQ-FIL-039.3`, and `REQ-FIL-043` across `PhoneCarriageClassifier.kt`, `MultisportTransitionDetector.kt`, `MultisportTransitionBridge.kt`, and `SpeedAndLocationDevice_ATTFused.kt`.
2. *Historical Origin & Commit Trace*: `fee3e805` (`ATT-8890`), `a93f3230` (`ATT-8917`), `0210b827` (`ATT-9062`), `6d80345c` (`ATT-9157`), `ATT-9347`, `18c199e7` (`ATT-9469`).
3. *Root Reason for Existing Formulation*: `+3.8` glance bonus ensured `0 ms` running wrist glances; time-only `3 s` / `15 s` windows kept `MultisportTransitionDetector` simple for clean triathlon transitions.
4. *Preservation of Core Invariants*: Preserves `Sport = RUN` tests (`TST-FIL-035.1`), trail step-batching `impactRms >= 1.8` protection (`TST-FIL-039.1`), `0 ms` running wrist glances, `ATT-9469` fast `STATIONARY` exit, `T1`/`T2` retroactive anchoring (incl. slow `2.0 m/s` joggers, parked bike sensors & phone-only duathlons), `@JvmOverloads` compatibility, `<= 1,500` LoC caps, and `0` edits to `TrackerService`/`BANALService`/UI/strings.

### REQ-FIL-045: Post-Workout ERTS Pause-Gap Zero-Distance Integration & Segment-Independent EKF/RTS Decoupling (`ATT-9242`) [Verified]
- **Target Release**: `V5.0.0`
- **Tests**: `TST-FIL-045`
- **System Requirement(s)**: `SYSREQ-TRK-003`, `SYSREQ-TRK-004`, `SYSREQ-TRK-005` — Refines post-workout ERTS timing reconstruction, 5-state odometry smoothing, and 3-state kinematic altitude/slope smoothing across auto-pause and manual pause gaps (`ErtsPostWorkoutRecomputer.kt`, `ExtendedRtsOdometrySmoother.kt`, `ExtendedRtsAltitudeSlopeSmoother.kt`).
- **Priority**: High
- **Type**: Functional
- **Verification Method**: Unit
- **Implementation**: `ErtsPostWorkoutRecomputer.kt`, `ExtendedRtsOdometrySmoother.kt`, `ExtendedRtsAltitudeSlopeSmoother.kt`
- **Localization Parity**: `N/A - 0 string changes`
- **Rationale**: During Pixel 10 ride `Zum Bäcker #59` (`workoutID = 2419`, `391 s` active / `570 s` total), live tracking paused cleanly (`DISTANCE_m_att_fused = 1,948.17 m`), but `ExtendedRtsOdometrySmoother.smoothSnapshots()` multiplied resume speed by the full wall-clock pause (`3.84 m/s * 134 s = +529.5 m` at row 218; `0.88 m/s * 17 s = +15.9 m` at row 199), inflating total distance by `+465 m` (`+24%`) to `2,413.42 m` and `speedAverage_mps` to `6.17 m/s`, while bridging forward EKF and backward RTS across pause gaps in both odometry and altitude smoothers (`ATT-9242`).
- **Description**:
  1. *Pause-Aware Timing Reconstruction & Recomputer Integration (`REQ-FIL-045.1`)*: `ErtsPostWorkoutRecomputer.readWorkoutSamples()` SHALL read `SensorType.TIME_ACTIVE` alongside `SensorType.TIME_TOTAL` and `time`. `reconstructTimingWithPauses()` SHALL detect pause boundaries at row `i > 0` via wall pause `(parsedTs - oneHzTs) > 5_000L` (overflow-safe), `TIME_TOTAL` pause `extraGapSec >= 5L`, OR paired `TIME_TOTAL`/`TIME_ACTIVE` divergence `deltaTotal > 0L && deltaActive >= 0L && (deltaTotal - deltaActive) > 2L && extraPairGapSec >= 2L` (`extraPairGapSec = deltaTotal - (i - prevPairIdx)`), advancing `ts` via `timeActivePauseTs = oneHzTs + extraPairGapSec * 1000L` merged with `max(wallPauseTs, timeTotalPauseTs, timeActivePauseTs)` (keeping `oneHzTs` synchronized across repeated `3–4 s` auto-pauses while remaining immune to `{0, 2}` clock-beat skew, constant `TIME_ACTIVE` with mid-stream nulls, and `<= 6 s` SQLite batch-flush stalls), and resetting `prevPairTotalSec = null; prevPairActiveSec = null; prevPairIdx = -1` whenever `pauseGapMask[i]` fires from any branch with `(timeTotalSec == null || timeActiveSec == null)`. `ErtsPostWorkoutRecomputer` SHALL propagate `isPauseGap` into `RawSampleRow`, `RawOdometrySample`, and `RawAltitudeSample`, reset `BarometricAltitudeFilter` at pause boundaries, run `detectAndCompensateBaroResetSteps()` independently per pause-delimited contiguous segment (blocking null-baro masking, cross-pause `preVals`/`postVals`, and Segment 1 `cumulativeShift` leakage while preserving `<= 5 s` non-pause crash-recovery steps), avoid crossing pause boundaries in `inferLegacyCarriageModeAt()`, and pass `pauseGapMask` to `computeAuthoritativeAverageSpeedMps()` in BOTH `updateWorkoutSummaries()` (`:801`) and `updateExtrema()` (`:916`) plus `!rawRows[i].isPauseGap` in `estimateRawReferenceDistanceMeters()`.
  2. *Segment-Independent Odometry ERTS Smoothing & Zero Pause Distance (`REQ-FIL-045.2`)*: `ExtendedRtsOdometrySmoother.smoothSamples()` SHALL partition `samples` into contiguous active segments at every pause boundary (`sample.isPauseGap || isTimestampPauseGap(prevTs, currTs)` with overflow-safe `MAX_ACTIVE_SAMPLE_GAP_MS = 5_000L`) and run initial-heading resolution, yaw-rate filtering, glitch pre-gating, forward EKF, transition speed envelope, and backward RTS independently per segment in the shared global `(refLat, refLon)` frame (preserving `result.size == samples.size`, no-GNSS segment fallback to prior ENU/heading, and 1-sample segments). `resolveInitialHeadingRad()` SHALL compute relative chord `(dE = eastM - firstEast, dN = northM - firstNorth)` from `segment[firstValidIdx]` (never global `(0, 0)`) and apply caller `explicitHeadingRad` strictly to Segment 0 (`Double.NaN` for Segment 1+) to prevent 180° heading inversion on out-and-back rides. `ExtendedRtsOdometrySmoother.smoothSnapshots()` SHALL accept `@JvmOverloads pauseGapMask: BooleanArray? = null`, decouple backward RTS (`C_k = 0`) across every pause boundary, and emit `deltaDistanceMeters = 0.0` at `k == 0` and every pause boundary `k`.
  3. *Segment-Independent Altitude/Slope ERTS Smoothing & Ascent/Descent Continuity (`REQ-FIL-045.3`)*: `ExtendedRtsAltitudeSlopeSmoother.smooth()` SHALL partition `samples` into contiguous active segments at every pause boundary (`sample.isPauseGap || isTimestampPauseGap(prevTs, currTs)` with `MAX_ACTIVE_SAMPLE_GAP_MS = 5_000L`) and run forward EKF and backward RTS independently per segment (`deltaDist = 0.0`, `verticalSpeedMps = 0.0` at resume). For BOTH `n == 1` and `n > 1` segments, `smoothContiguousSegment()` SHALL preserve prior `ascentOffset`/`descentOffset` monotonically (never resetting `ASCENDING`/`DESCENDING` to `0.0` on a trailing 1-sample segment), re-anchor per-segment hysteresis to the segment's first smoothed altitude, fall back to `fallbackInitialAlt` (never `0.0`) when all altitudes are `NaN`, scan for the first finite `rawAltitudeM` when applying `rawAlt - inheritedBaroBias` on baro-only (`!hasReference && hasBaroData`) segments, and forward `inheritedBaroBias` unchanged across `n == 1` and `!hasBaroData` (DEM-only) segments.

**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:
• *Given* a workout recording with a `134 s` auto-pause gap (`TIME_TOTAL: 241 -> 375 s`, `TIME_ACTIVE: 218 -> 219 s`, resume speed `3.84 m/s`, `Zum Bäcker #59` profile), *When* `ErtsPostWorkoutRecomputer` and `ExtendedRtsOdometrySmoother.smoothSamples()` execute, *Then* `deltaDistanceMeters` across the pause row is `0.0 m` (never `+529.5 m`), total smoothed distance stays within `±3%` of live `DISTANCE_m_att_fused` (`~1,948 m`), and `speedAverage_mps` reflects true active moving average (`~5.0 m/s`, not `6.17 m/s`).
• *Given* a workout with multiple auto-pauses (`4 s`, `17 s`, `134 s`) and manual pauses (`> 10 min`), *When* odometry and altitude ERTS smoothers run, *Then* forward/backward RTS states are decoupled across every pause boundary with zero phantom distance, zero phantom altitude jump, and full `Laps.db` / `DISTANCE_m_LAP` parity.

**Chesterton's Fence (`REQ-PRO-022`)**:
1. *Original Requirement ID & Target*: Net-new `REQ-FIL-045` refining `REQ-FIL-017.1`–`.3`, `REQ-FIL-027.1`, and `REQ-FIL-032.1` across `ErtsPostWorkoutRecomputer.kt`, `ExtendedRtsOdometrySmoother.kt`, and `ExtendedRtsAltitudeSlopeSmoother.kt`.
2. *Historical Origin & Commit Trace*: `ATT-7750`, `ATT-8705`, `ATT-8812`/`ATT-8880` (`REQ-FIL-027.1`), `ATT-9064` (`REQ-FIL-032.1`–`.3`), `ATT-9063` (`REQ-FIL-033`), `ATT-9195` (`REQ-FIL-036.3`), `ATT-9359` (`REQ-FIL-040.4`), `REQ-STB-025.4`.
3. *Root Reason for Existing Formulation*: Original ERTS assumed continuous 1-Hz active tracks (`deltaDist = v * dt`); `TST-FIL-027.1` used `deltaDist > 100 m` across synthetic 30 s/25 s pauses as an indirect timestamp-preservation proxy.
4. *Preservation of Core Invariants*: Preserves strict canonical column whitelist (`ALLOWED_CANONICAL_COLUMNS`), untouched device-attributed `" ("` columns, Null-Island `NULL` preservation, `{0, 2}` clock-beat & `6 s` SQLite batch-flush stall immunity (updating only the obsolete `TST-FIL-027.1` pause-distance proxy to assert direct timestamp preservation + `0.0 m` pause distance), indoor-stop ZUPT pinning, delayed DEM convergence, `<= 5 s` crash-recovery baro reset compensation, `@JvmOverloads` compatibility, `<= 1,500` LoC caps, and `0` string changes.

### REQ-SEN-035: `EditEquipmentDialog` Wheel Mounting Position Prompt, Display & Atomic Persistence (`ATT-9320`) [Verified]
- **Target Release**: `V5.0.0`
- **Tests**: `TST-SEN-035`
- **System Requirement(s)**: `SYSREQ-TRK-001`, `SYSREQ-TRK-003` — Extends Front/Rear wheel mounting position parity (`REQ-SEN-029`, `REQ-SEN-031`, `REQ-SEN-032`) to bike creation and editing in `EditEquipmentDialog.kt`, `EquipmentTabsScreen.kt`, `EquipmentViewModel.kt`, `EquipmentRepository.kt`, and `EquipmentDbHelper.java`.
- **Priority**: Medium
- **Type**: Functional
- **Verification Method**: Unit
- **Implementation**: `EditEquipmentDialog.kt`, `EquipmentTabsScreen.kt`, `EquipmentViewModel.kt`, `EquipmentRepository.kt`, `EquipmentDbHelper.java`
- **Localization Parity**: `N/A - 0 string changes`
- **Rationale**: PO sprint review of `ATT-8859` identified that while `EquipmentSensorMatrixScreen` and `EditDeviceDialog` prompt for `Front`/`Rear` mounting position via `WheelPositionSelectionDialog`, `EditEquipmentDialog` silently saved newly linked wheel speed sensors as `WheelPosition.UNSPECIFIED` without displaying or letting the user edit their mounting position (`ATT-9320`).
- **Description**:
  1. *Wheel Speed Sensor Prompt & Conflict-Swap Staging (`REQ-SEN-035.1`)*: In `EditEquipmentDialog`, when adding or editing a bike (`item.frameType > 0`), tapping a wheel speed sensor (`DeviceType.BIKE_SPEED` or `DeviceType.BIKE_SPEED_AND_CADENCE`) in `MultiSelectSensorSpinner` SHALL open the existing `WheelPositionSelectionDialog` (`Front Wheel` / `Rear Wheel`, dynamic `conflictHints` from other currently selected wheel sensors on the bike, and `Unlink Sensor` when already linked). Selecting a position already occupied by another selected wheel sensor SHALL apply conflict-swap resolution in draft state (swapping positions between 2 sensors, moving to the free opposite wheel, or falling back to `UNSPECIFIED` if a 3rd wheel sensor is linked and both slots are occupied) with zero duplicate `FRONT`/`REAR` assignments; unlinking removes the sensor and its draft position; dismissing discards changes.
  2. *Visible Mounting Position Display & Non-Wheel Sensor Passthrough (`REQ-SEN-035.2`)*: `MultiSelectSensorSpinner` on bikes SHALL render assigned wheel positions in both the collapsed `OutlinedTextField` summary (`"SensorName (Front Wheel)"` / `"SensorName (Rear Wheel)"` / `"SensorName (Unspecified Position)"`) and on each wheel speed sensor row inside the dropdown (position subtitle + compact `F`/`R` / `V`/`H` badge pill matching `EditDeviceDialog`), reusing existing 9-locale `strings_equipment.xml` resources (`0` new strings). Non-wheel-speed sensors (HR, Power, Cadence-only, Radar) and shoe equipment (`frameType <= 0`) SHALL retain simple 1-tap checkbox toggling without prompting or mutating other sensors' wheel positions.
  3. *Atomic `addEquipment`/`updateEquipment` Persistence & Preservation (`REQ-SEN-035.3`)*: `EquipmentTabsScreen`, `EquipmentViewModel`, `EquipmentRepository`, and `EquipmentDbHelper` SHALL persist `linkedDeviceWheelPositions` atomically inside the `addEquipment` and `updateEquipment` SQLite transactions via backward-compatible overloads (preserving existing 3-arg `addEquipment` and 5-arg `updateEquipment` signatures and legacy test mocks), preserving previously persisted `WheelPosition` values for untouched already-linked sensors and emitting `EquipmentRepository.notifyEquipmentLinksChanged(null)`.

**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:
• *Given* a user opens `EditEquipmentDialog` to add or edit a bike with paired wheel speed sensors (`BIKE_SPEED` / `BIKE_SPEED_AND_CADENCE`) and non-wheel sensors (HR/Power), *When* they tap a wheel speed sensor, *Then* `WheelPositionSelectionDialog` opens with `Front`/`Rear` options (plus conflict swap hint if another sensor holds that slot, and `Unlink` if already linked), updates the visible position label/badge on selection, and persists the exact positions on Save without overwriting untouched sensor positions.
• *Given* a user taps a non-wheel-speed sensor (HR, Power, Cadence-only, Radar) or edits shoes (`frameType == 0`), *When* they toggle the sensor, *Then* it toggles directly without opening `WheelPositionSelectionDialog`.

**Chesterton's Fence (`REQ-PRO-022`)**:
1. *Original Requirement ID & Target*: Net-new `REQ-SEN-035` extending `REQ-SEN-029`, `REQ-SEN-031`, and `REQ-SEN-032` to `EditEquipmentDialog`.
2. *Historical Origin & Commit Trace*: `ATT-7752`, `b08c779e` (`ATT-8719`), `2d7f1489` (`ATT-8859`).
3. *Root Reason for Existing Formulation*: `EditEquipmentDialog` predated `WHEEL_POSITION` tracking and only passed `List<Long>` `linkedDeviceIds`.
4. *Preservation of Core Invariants*: Preserves `EditEquipmentDialogContractTest` (`compatibleSensors`, `allSensors = compatibleSensors`), `EquipmentViewModelRetiredTest` 5-arg mock behavior, `WheelPositionSelectionDialog` Front/Rear-only contract (`ATT-8859`), untouched sensor position preservation in SQLite, `<= 1,500` LoC file caps, and `0` string changes.
### REQ-TRK-096: Race-Free Monotonic `LAP_NR` Enforcement & Finalization Reconciliation Across Retroactive Multisport Backfills (`ATT-9468`) [Verified]
- **Target Release**: `V5.0.0`
- **Tests**: `TST-TRK-096`
- **System Requirement(s)**: `SYSREQ-TRK-003`, `SYSREQ-TRK-005` — Ensures deterministic 1-Hz sample `LAP_NR` monotonicity across concurrent retroactive multisport transitions and background service finalization (`MultisportLapCoordinator.kt`, `TrackerService.java`).
- **Priority**: High
- **Type**: Functional
- **Verification Method**: Unit
- **Implementation**: `MultisportLapCoordinator.kt`, `TrackerService.java`, `docs/architecture/detailed_design/tracker_service.md`
- **Localization Parity**: `N/A - 0 string changes`
- **Rationale**: In Pixel 10 workout `2026-11-21_081228` (`workoutID = 2423`, `row 425`, `TIME_TOTAL = 1353 s`, `08:35:01`), `TrackerService.sampleAndWriteToDb()` snapshotted stale `LAP_NR = 4` on `mScheduler` before `MultisportLapCoordinator.onMultisportTransitionIntent()` bumped live `LAP_NR` to `6` and enqueued `backfillWorkoutSamplesLapNumbers()` onto `mDbExecutor`. Because the in-flight sample flushed onto `mDbExecutor` after the backfill `UPDATE` committed, `row 425` landed permanently with `LAP_NR = 4` inside `Lap 6`, corrupting SQLite, CSV/FIT/TCX exports, lap charts, and `ErtsPostWorkoutRecomputer`. Additionally, multi-step `BANALService.advanceLapsForMultisport()` could expose a torn intermediate `LAP_NR = 5` unmatched by `LAP_NR = oldLapNr` (`ATT-9468`, extending `REQ-FIL-031.3` and `REQ-TRK-090`).
- **Description**:
  1. *Committed Multisport Transition Windows & Decoupled Sample/Summary Monotonic Enforcement (`REQ-TRK-096.1`)*: Whenever `MultisportLapCoordinator.planAndCommitAnchorTransition()` commits a transition with `lapAdvanceCount > 0`, it SHALL atomically (under its leaf-only `@Synchronized` monitor, BEFORE `advanceLapsFn()` and `persistTask` submission) append an immutable `CommittedTransitionWindow(baseFileName, exitTotalS, enterTotalS, exitEpochS, enterEpochS, hasTransition, transitionLapNr, newLegLapNr)` to `committedTransitions` (and to a bounded `<= 4`-entry `pendingReconciliationByFileName` map when `baseFileName` is non-blank) and set `@Volatile hasCommittedTransitions = true`. `MultisportLapCoordinator` SHALL expose `@JvmOverloads fun enforceMonotonicLapNr(baseFileName: String?, samplingValues: ContentValues, summaryValues: ContentValues? = null, sampleEpochS: Long = System.currentTimeMillis() / 1000L)`, invoked by `TrackerService.sampleAndWriteToDb()` on `mDbExecutor` immediately before `WorkoutRepository.recordLiveSample()`. When `hasCommittedTransitions` is true, for all matching windows (`w.baseFileName == null || baseFileName == null || w.baseFileName == baseFileName`): (a) per-sample `samplingValues[LAP_NR]` SHALL resolve `requiredLapNr` against `samplingValues[TIME_TOTAL]` (falling back to UTC `time` or `sampleEpochS` when `TIME_TOTAL` is absent) — requiring `transitionLapNr` on `[exit, enter)` (iff `hasTransition && enter > exit`) and `newLegLapNr` on `>= enter` — upgrading `samplingValues[LAP_NR]` iff `null` or `< requiredLapNr` (never downgrading); and (b) workout-wide `summaryValues[WorkoutSummaries.LAPS]` (whenever `summaryValues != null && summaryValues.containsKey(WorkoutSummaries.LAPS)`) SHALL be floored against `maxCommittedNewLegLapNr = matchingWindows.maxOf { it.newLegLapNr }` (never downgrading higher manual laps) so delayed `< exitS` or `[exitS, enterS)` samples flushing out of order never regress `WorkoutSummaries.LAPS`. `resetForSession()` SHALL clear active `committedTransitions` and reset `hasCommittedTransitions = false`.
  2. *Combined `TIME_TOTAL IS NULL`-Safe Backfill Predicates & Mixed-Window Finalization Reconciliation (`REQ-TRK-096.2`)*: `MultisportLapCoordinator.backfillWorkoutSamplesTable()` SHALL share a single transaction-scoped helper `applyTransitionWindowsToTable()` using strict monotonic predicates `($lapNrCol < ? OR $lapNrCol IS NULL)` for both `[exit, enter)` (`transitionLapNr`) and `>= enter` (`newLegLapNr`): when `hasTimeTotal && hasTimeCol`, use combined predicates `(($timeTotalCol >= ? AND $timeTotalCol < ?) OR ($timeTotalCol IS NULL AND CAST(strftime('%s', $timeCol) AS INTEGER) >= ? AND CAST(strftime('%s', $timeCol) AS INTEGER) < ?))` (respecting `enterTotalS > exitTotalS` / `enterEpochS > exitEpochS`) and `($timeTotalCol >= ? OR ($timeTotalCol IS NULL AND CAST(strftime('%s', $timeCol) AS INTEGER) >= ?))` while preserving single-column branches for schema-minimal tables, upgrading stale `oldLapNr`, torn `oldLapNr + 1`, and `NULL` without ever downgrading higher manual laps. `MultisportLapCoordinator.reconcileWorkoutSamplesOnFinalization(context: Context, baseFileName: String?)` (invoked on `mDbExecutor` inside `TrackerService.endWorkout()` immediately after `createNewLap()` and before `finalizeLiveSession()` / `WorkoutPostProcessingCoordinator.enqueue()`) SHALL short-circuit immediately on `baseFileName.isNullOrBlank()`, merge `pendingReconciliationByFileName.remove(baseFileName)` with any matching `baseFileName == null` (or matching `baseFileName`) windows in `committedTransitions` deduplicated in order (`LinkedHashSet`), return with `0` SQLite queries when empty, and otherwise replay all merged windows inside a fault-isolated `try/catch (Throwable)` transaction.
  3. *Lock Ordering, Zero Main-Thread SQLite, LoC Ceilings & `SWE.3` Parity (`REQ-TRK-096.3`)*: `MultisportLapCoordinator`'s monitor MUST remain a pure in-memory leaf lock (never calling `BANALService`, `WorkoutRepository`, or `SQLiteDatabase` while synchronized) with zero main-thread SQLite I/O, backward-compatible `@JvmOverloads` signatures, `0` edits to `WorkoutSamplesDatabaseManager.java`/`BANALService.java`/`DeviceManager.java`/`WorkoutRepository.kt`, `TrackerService.java <= 3,220` LoC, `MultisportLapCoordinator.kt <= 1,500` LoC, and synchronized `docs/architecture/detailed_design/tracker_service.md` (`REQ-PRO-114`).

**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:
• *Given* a 1-Hz sample at `t = confirmTime` (`TIME_TOTAL = 1353 s`) snapshotted `oldLapNr = 4` (or torn `5`) when `MULTISPORT_TRANSITION_INTENT` advances `4 -> 6`, *When* `onMultisportTransitionIntent()` executes `advanceLapsFn()` and `backfillWorkoutSamplesLapNumbers()` concurrently with `TrackerService.sampleAndWriteToDb()`, *Then* the persisted row at `t = 1353 s` has `LAP_NR = 6` (never `4` or `5`), `summaryValues.LAPS = 6`, and `row[i].LAP_NR <= row[i+1].LAP_NR` holds across `WorkoutSamples.db`.
• *Given* multiple consecutive multisport transitions (`Run -> T1 -> Bike -> manual lap -> T2 -> Run`) under concurrent 1-Hz writes and rapid Stop/Start sessions, *When* transitions complete and `endWorkout()` finishes, *Then* every `[exitS, enterS)` row carries `transitionLapNr`, every `>= enterS` row carries `newLegLapNr` (or a higher manual/subsequent lap), and zero orphaned older `LAP_NR` rows or cross-session leaks occur.

**Chesterton's Fence (`REQ-PRO-022`)**:
1. *Original Requirement ID & Target*: Net-new `REQ-TRK-096` extending `REQ-FIL-031.3` (`ATT-8917`) and `REQ-TRK-090` (`ATT-9159`) across `MultisportLapCoordinator.kt` and `TrackerService.java`.
2. *Historical Origin & Commit Trace*: `a93f3230` (`ATT-8917`) split synchronous `advanceLapsFn()` from async `mDbExecutor` `backfillWorkoutSamplesLapNumbers()` to preserve zero main-thread SQLite and zero edits to `WorkoutSamplesDatabaseManager.java`.
3. *Root Reason for Existing Formulation*: Kept live cockpit updates immediate while avoiding main-thread SQLite and protecting later manual laps via `LAP_NR = oldLapNr`.
4. *Preservation of Core Invariants*: Preserves zero main-thread SQLite, synchronous `advanceLapsFn()`, manual-lap non-downgrade protection, `REQ-TRK-002` phantom-lap guard, pause/resume in-memory leg retention, `0` edits to `WorkoutSamplesDatabaseManager.java`/`BANALService.java`/`DeviceManager.java`/`WorkoutRepository.kt`, `TrackerService.java <= 3,220` LoC, and `0` string changes.

### REQ-TRK-097: Dynamic 9-Language Re-Localization of Unmodified Default Tracking Cockpit Tab Titles (`ATT-9511`) [Verified]
- **Target Release**: `V5.0.0`
- **Tests**: `TST-TRK-097`
- **System Requirement(s)**: `SYSREQ-TRK-006` — Ensures Tracking Cockpit tab headers localize consistently across all 9 supported languages whenever the device/app locale changes (`TrackingTabTitleResolver.kt`, `TrackingViewsRepository.kt`, `TrackingTabsScreen.kt`).
- **Priority**: Medium
- **Type**: Functional
- **Verification Method**: Unit
- **Implementation**: `TrackingTabTitleResolver.kt`, `TrackingViewsRepository.kt`, `TrackingTabsScreen.kt`, `TrackingTabPreviewHeader.kt`, `TrackingTabConfigHeader.kt`
- **Localization Parity**: `N/A - 0 string changes` (reuses existing 9-locale `text_default`, `tab_power`, `tab_map`, `tab_laps`)
- **Rationale**: Observed in the 2026-KW47 9-language emulator sweep (`ATT-9386`, `docs/engineering/emulator_sweeps/2026-11-21_sweep.md`), `TrackingViewsDatabaseManager.TrackingViewsDbHelper.onCreate()` persists localized strings (`R.string.text_default`, `R.string.tab_power`, `R.string.tab_map`, `R.string.tab_laps`) into SQLite `ViewsTable.Name` at DB creation. When the device/app locale changes later (e.g., `de -> es/ja/pt`), Tab 1 localizes via `stringResource(R.string.tab_start)`, while Tabs 2–4 remain stuck in the initial persisted SQLite strings (`"Standard"`, `"Karte"`, `"Runden"`), compounded by `TrackingTabsViewModel.trackingViews` (`WhileSubscribed(5000)`) surviving Activity recreation (`ATT-9511`).
- **Description**:
  1. *Canonical 9-Language Default Tab Key Mapping (`REQ-TRK-097.1`)*: `TrackingTabTitleResolver` SHALL define `DefaultTrackingTabKey` (`DEFAULT -> R.string.text_default`, `POWER -> R.string.tab_power`, `MAP -> R.string.tab_map`, `LAPS -> R.string.tab_laps`) and `resolveDefaultTabKey(persistedName: String?)` mapping every exact unmodified default tab title across all 9 supported locales (`en`, `de`, `es`, `fr`, `it`, `ja`, `nl`, `pl`, `pt`) deterministically to its `DefaultTrackingTabKey`, returning `null` for any user-renamed custom title or numeric empty tab (`"4"`).
  2. *Repository & Compose Dynamic Locale Resolution (`REQ-TRK-097.2`)*: `TrackingViewsRepository.toTrackingViewInfo()` SHALL resolve default titles via `TrackingTabTitleResolver.resolveTitle(context, rawName)` (preserving `rawName` fallback if `getString()` is empty/unavailable in mocked tests), and `TrackingTabsScreen.kt`, `TrackingTabPreviewHeader.kt`, and `TrackingTabConfigHeader.kt` SHALL resolve tab titles via `@Composable fun resolveTrackingTabTitle(name: String)` using `stringResource(key.titleResId)` so locale changes update all unmodified default tab titles immediately even across retained `TrackingTabsViewModel` state (`TrackingTabConfigHeader` keying `localName` on `(viewInfo.tabViewId, currentLocale)`).
  3. *Custom Title Preservation & Schema/LoC Parity (`REQ-TRK-097.3`)*: User-renamed custom tab titles (e.g. `"Intervalle"`, `"Race Cockpit"`) and numeric empty tabs SHALL remain 100% preserved across locale switches; `TrackingViewsDatabaseManager.DB_VERSION = 22` and `ensurePowerTabExistsForBikePower()` query behavior remain untouched; `TrackingTabsScreen.kt <= 1,500` LoC; `0` string resource edits (`N/A - 0 string changes`).

**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:
• *Given* default tracking tabs initialized in SQLite under locale A (e.g. German `"Standard"`, `"Leistung"`, `"Karte"`, `"Runden"` — either newly created or legacy persisted rows), *When* the user switches device/app locale to locale B across all 9 supported languages (`en`, `de`, `es`, `fr`, `it`, `ja`, `nl`, `pl`, `pt`), *Then* unmodified default tracking tab titles render in locale B across repository reads, Cockpit tab bar, preview header, and config header.
• *Given* a user-renamed custom tab title (e.g. `"Intervalle"` or `"Race Cockpit"`) or numeric empty tab (`"4"`), *When* the user switches device/app locale to locale B, *Then* the custom title remains 100% unchanged.

### REQ-TRK-098: Full-Screen Radar Top-Down ("GTA-1 Style") Overhead Vehicle Markers & Expert Settings Style Selector [Verified]
- **Target Release**: `V5.0.0`
- **Tests**: `TST-TRK-098`
- **System Requirement(s)**: `SYSREQ-TRK-004` — Provides optional overhead top-down vehicle visualization in Full-Screen Rearview Radar mode selectable in Expert Settings (`RadarTopDownVehicleMarker.kt`, `RadarEdgeOverlay.kt`, `RadarSettingsSection.kt`, `TuningPreferencesDataStore.kt`, `TrackingTabsScreen.kt`).
- **Priority**: Medium
- **Type**: Functional
- **Verification Method**: Unit
- **Implementation**: `RadarTopDownVehicleMarker.kt`, `RadarEdgeOverlay.kt`, `Color.kt`, `TuningPreferencesDataStore.kt`, `RadarSettingsSection.kt`, `TrackingTabsScreen.kt`, `strings_radar.xml`
- **Localization Parity**: `PASS - 9/9 locales (values*/strings_radar.xml: radar_fullscreen_marker_style_title, radar_fullscreen_marker_style_badge, radar_fullscreen_marker_style_top_down, radar_fullscreen_marker_style_badge_desc, radar_fullscreen_marker_style_top_down_desc)`
- **Rationale**: Follow-up feature from Pixel 10 Sprint Review of `ATT-8733` (`ATT-8959`, Epic `ATT-1286`, complementing `REQ-TRK-089` and `REQ-TRK-062`). Rendering approaching vehicles as intuitive overhead ("GTA-1 style") car and truck silhouettes aligned with the cyclist's forward travel direction (`bottom -> top`) gives athletes an immediate spatial mental model while letting them choose between *Compact Badge* (`ATT-8958`) and *Top-Down Vehicles* in Expert Settings.
- **Description**:
  1. *Persistent Full-Screen Marker Style Setting & 9-Locale Expert Settings Selector (`REQ-TRK-098.1`)*:
     - `TuningPreferencesDataStore` SHALL define `RadarFullScreenMarkerStyle` (`COMPACT_BADGE`, `TOP_DOWN_VEHICLES`), default `TuningPreferencesDefaults.RADAR_FULL_SCREEN_MARKER_STYLE = RadarFullScreenMarkerStyle.COMPACT_BADGE`, `KEY_RADAR_FULL_SCREEN_MARKER_STYLE` (`"tuning_radar_full_screen_marker_style"`), `TuningConfig.radarFullScreenMarkerStyle`, `radarFullScreenMarkerStyleFlow`, and `updateRadarFullScreenMarkerStyle()` with defensive `safeEnumValueOf` fallback and factory-reset cleanup.
     - `RadarSettingsSection` SHALL display a `TuningSegmentedButtonRow` (`COMPACT_BADGE` vs `TOP_DOWN_VEHICLES`, localized across all 9 `values*/strings_radar.xml` locales) strictly when `tuningConfig.radarEdgeSide == RadarEdgeSide.FULL_SCREEN`, persisting selection immediately to `TuningPreferencesDataStore` and forwarding it from `TrackingTabsScreen` into `RadarEdgeOverlay`.
  2. *Overhead Top-Down ("GTA-1 Style") Canvas Vehicle Renderer (`REQ-TRK-098.2`)*:
     - Dedicated sibling file `RadarTopDownVehicleMarker.kt` (keeping `RadarEdgeOverlay.kt <= 1,500` LoC) SHALL draw upward-pointing (`bottom -> top`) overhead vehicle silhouettes using centralized `Color.kt` tokens (`TTColor.RadarTopDown*`, zero inline `Color(0x...)` in composables):
       • `UNKNOWN_OR_LIGHT`: compact overhead generic vehicle/hatchback (`46×72 dp`, **never** a motorcycle per `ATT-8958`) with bold `18.sp` `tnum` distance badge.
       • `CAR`: overhead 3-box sedan (`52×84 dp`, larger than `UNKNOWN_OR_LIGHT`) with headlights at top, angled windshield, roof panel, side mirrors, tires, rear glass, taillights at bottom, and bold `19.sp` `tnum` distance badge.
       • `TRUCK_OR_BUS`: elongated overhead cab + articulated cargo trailer with roof ribs (`60×114 dp`, largest silhouette) and bold `20.sp` `tnum` distance badge.
     - Distance readouts (`15m`, `128m`, `160m`) SHALL render unclipped in a high-contrast solid opaque (`alpha = 1.0f`) dual-rim capsule coupled with the vehicle silhouette inside a single-pass `.layout` Frame-1 bounds-clamped container (zero overflow at `0.15f`/`0.85f` horizontal and `0.0f`/`1.0f` vertical extremes).
  3. *Preserved Overlay, Anti-Oscillation & Touch Pass-Through Invariants (`REQ-TRK-098.3`)*:
     - `RadarTopDownVehicleMarker` MUST attach zero `.clickable`, `.pointerInput`, or `.pointerInteropFilter` modifiers (`REQ-TRK-062.3` 100% touch pass-through for `HorizontalPager` and `GoogleMap`).
     - All `ATT-8958` invariants (`hasFullScreen2DLateralData` V1 lane-guide suppression, 100% solid opaque non-pulsing threat color, per-target `stepThreatHysteresis`, clear-road badge suppression `ATT-8679`, untouched `LEFT`/`RIGHT` `RadarVerticalRail` `REQ-TRK-073.2`, `private` `@Preview` functions, `0` `src/main` test file reads) SHALL remain 100% preserved.

**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:
• *Given* `RadarEdgeSide.FULL_SCREEN` selected in `RadarSettingsSection`, *When* the athlete switches `RadarFullScreenMarkerStyle` between `COMPACT_BADGE` and `TOP_DOWN_VEHICLES`, *Then* the segmented selector persists immediately via `TuningPreferencesDataStore` (`safeEnumValueOf` fallback on corrupt/unknown storage) and `RadarEdgeOverlay` switches live between compact capsules and top-down overhead vehicle silhouettes (hiding the selector when `LEFT` or `RIGHT` edge rail is selected).
• *Given* `RadarFullScreenMarkerStyle.TOP_DOWN_VEHICLES` with `UNKNOWN_OR_LIGHT`, `CAR`, and `TRUCK_OR_BUS` targets at `15m`, `128m`, and `160m` near viewport boundaries, *When* rendered in Light, Dark, and Map backdrops, *Then* all three overhead silhouettes (`UNKNOWN_OR_LIGHT < CAR < TRUCK_OR_BUS`, zero motorcycles) and unclipped distance labels stay strictly inside container bounds on Frame 1, pass direct taps and horizontal swipes through to underlying siblings, suppress lane guides when `!has2DData`, and maintain 9/9 localization parity.
### REQ-CON-059: Immediate & Debounce-Consistent Manual-to-Automatic Radar Tail Light Activation (`ATT-8673`) [Verified]
- **Target Release**: `V5.0.0`
- **Tests**: `TST-CON-059`
- **System Requirement(s)**: `SYSREQ-TRK-006`, `SYSREQ-TRK-007` — Refines smart radar tail-light automatic mode evaluation, debounced quick-switch cycling, serialized preference persistence, and brake-light preemption safety (`RadarLightController.kt`, `SmartRadarLightEngine.kt`).
- **Priority**: High
- **Type**: Functional
- **Verification Method**: Unit
- **Implementation**: `RadarLightController.kt`, `SmartRadarLightEngine.kt`
- **Localization Parity**: `N/A - 0 string changes`
- **Rationale**: During Ceremony 2 on-device review (`ATT-8455`), switching from a manual radar tail light mode (`SOLID`, `PELOTON`, `DAY_FLASH`) back to `Automatik` left the tail light stuck on the previous manual mode (`ATT-8673`). Forensic audit against `HEAD` (`8efc52af`) identified six active root causes across `RadarLightController.kt` and `SmartRadarLightEngine.kt`: (1) `evaluateAndDispatch()` short-circuits on stale/offline-poisoned `lastDispatchedMode` and gates explicit `resumeAutomation()` behind the 1,500 ms traffic-oscillation rate-limit window; (2) `cycleNextMode()` transitioning `DAY_FLASH -> OFF (debounced) -> AUTO` within 400 ms cancels the unflushed `OFF` dispatch while `lastDispatchedMode == OFF` suppresses automatic hardware re-dispatch (and rapid `OFF -> AUTO -> PELOTON -> SOLID` emits an un-debounced write on `AUTO`); (3) `setAutoMode()` never calls `SmartRadarLightEngine.start(ctx)` when a cycling workout started in Manual/Fixed mode and never synchronizes `currentThreatStage` from live `RadarStateRepository.threatState` outside workouts; (4) `onManualOverride()` fails to cancel queued `rateLimitFlushRunnable` and `flushPendingDispatch()` omits `isAutomationEnabled`/`isManualOverridden`/`isBrakeActive` guards; (5) unserialized `persistSelectionAsync()` writes on `Dispatchers.IO` allow stale `isAuto = false` completions to clobber a newer `setAutoMode()` via `onPreferencesUpdated()`/`radarSmartLightAutomationFlow`; and (6) `checkAndRestorePreBrakeMode()` dispatches stale manual `preBrakeMode` behind rate-limiting and `SmartRadarLightEngine.stop()` leaks stale `lastDispatchedMode` across rides.
- **Description**:
  1. *Immediate Automatic Evaluation, Mid-Ride Engine Start & Paused-Sensor Battery Protection on `setAutoMode()` (`REQ-CON-059.1`)*: `RadarLightController.setAutoMode()` SHALL cancel pending debounce state, set `_controlMode = SMART_AUTOMATION` and `_isAutoMode = true`, synchronously arm `pendingPersistedAutoMode = true` BEFORE starting persistence/engine observers (`REQ-CON-059.3`), and (outside `controllerLock`) invoke `SmartRadarLightEngine.resumeAutomation()`. `resumeAutomation()` SHALL cancel queued `rateLimitFlushRunnable`, sync `currentThreatStage` with live `RadarStateRepository.threatState.value`, clear `isManualOverridden = false`, enable `isAutomationEnabled = true` without duplicate evaluation, and immediately evaluate & dispatch `computeTargetMode(isTunnelActive, currentSolarProfile, currentThreatStage)` (`REQ-CON-034`: Daytime `OFF -> SOLID -> DAY_FLASH`; Night/Tunnel `PELOTON -> SOLID -> NIGHT_FLASH`; StVZO clamped to `OFF`/`SOLID`), bypassing the 1,500 ms autonomous rate-limit window. When `isWorkoutActive && isCyclingSession && appContext != null`, `setAutoMode()` SHALL invoke `SmartRadarLightEngine.start(ctx)` outside `controllerLock`. `SmartRadarLightEngine` SHALL track `@Volatile isWorkoutPaused` (set via `onWorkoutPaused()`, cleared in `start()`/`stop()`/`reset()`) and gate `startListening()` strictly on `isWorkoutActive && !isWorkoutPaused && appContext != null` so calling `setAutoMode()` while paused still dispatches automatic baseline without registering `TYPE_LIGHT`/`TYPE_PROXIMITY` sensors. `RadarStateRepository.lightState` SHALL reflect `(isAutoMode = true, lightMode = _currentMode.value)` while preserving offline gated mutation (`REQ-CON-031.2`, `REQ-CON-048.2`).
  2. *Deferred-Persistence Debounced `cycleNextMode()` (`OFF -> AUTO`), Post-Stop Non-Resuscitation & Truthful `lastDispatchedMode` (`REQ-CON-059.2`)*: `RadarLightController.cycleNextMode()` stepping `OFF -> AUTO` (`applyDebouncedAutoStep()`) SHALL update UI state immediately (`isAutoMode = true`, previewing StVZO-resolved `previewTargetMode()` when connected/simulating & not braking) while deferring BOTH hardware activation AND `persistSelectionAsync()` strictly into `flushDebouncedDispatch()` (`DEBOUNCE_DELAY_MS = 400 ms`), ensuring rapid `OFF -> AUTO -> PELOTON -> SOLID` emits strictly 1 hardware write (`SOLID`, zero premature `editAndSync` dispatch) while stopping on `AUTO` (including `DAY_FLASH -> OFF (debounced) -> AUTO`) flushes immediate `resumeAutomation()`, mid-ride `start(ctx)`, and `persistSelectionAsync()`. `onWorkoutStopped()` SHALL call `SmartRadarLightEngine.stop()` (disabling `isAutomationEnabled = false` without dispatch, clearing `lastDispatchedMode = null`/`pendingModeToDispatch = null`, cancelling `rateLimitFlushRunnable`) and resolve control mode via `resolveActiveConfig(context, syncEngine = false)` so stopping a workout NEVER re-enables automation or resuscitates the tail light after `OFF`. `dispatchMode()` SHALL record `lastDispatchedMode` strictly on `committed == true` (clearing to `null` on failure/offline); non-forced `evaluateAndDispatch()` compares both `lastDispatchedMode` and live `currentMode.value` (retaining `>= 1,500 ms` rate-limiting); `onManualOverride()` cancels `rateLimitFlushRunnable`; `flushPendingDispatch()` re-checks `isAutomationEnabled && !isManualOverridden && !RadarLightController.isBrakeActive`.
  3. *Explicit Pending-Persistence Superseding State Machine & External Preference Ordering (`REQ-CON-059.3`)*: `RadarLightController` SHALL maintain `persistenceMutex: Mutex`, `pendingPersistedAutoMode: Boolean?`, and `pendingPersistedGeneration: Long` (armed synchronously prior to `persistenceScope.launch` ONLY when a real DataStore write is scheduled, cleared in `finally` iff `pendingPersistedGeneration == gen`, and cleared in `reset()`). `persistSelectionAsync()` SHALL acquire `persistenceMutex` and drop superseded generations (`gen != selectionGeneration.get()`). `internal fun shouldIgnoreStaleDataStoreAutoMode(observedAutoMode: Boolean): Boolean` (`pendingPersistedAutoMode != null && observedAutoMode != pendingPersistedAutoMode`) SHALL guard BOTH `RadarLightController.onPreferencesUpdated()` and `SmartRadarLightEngine`'s `radarSmartLightAutomationFlow.collectLatest` against stale pre-write `false` emissions (e.g., mid-ride `start(ctx)` initial emission or unrelated `editAndSync`) while keeping external `TuningPreferencesDataStore` updates (`pendingPersistedAutoMode == null`) 100% immediate (`REQ-CON-056.2`). External `onPreferencesUpdated()` with `isAuto == true` MUST clear `isManualOverridden = false` strictly BEFORE setting `isAutomationEnabled = true`.
  4. *Brake-Light Preemption Atomicity, Leaf-Only Disjoint Locks & Structural Invariants (`REQ-CON-059.4`)*: Active brake illumination (`RadarLightController.isBrakeActive == true`) SHALL NEVER be preempted; `RadarLightController.setAutomationLightMode()` SHALL gate BOTH `RadarStateRepository.updateLightState` and its boolean return value strictly on `committed == true` (`!isBrakeActive && brakeEpoch == expectedEpoch && selectionGeneration.get() == expectedGen`). On 2.0 s post-brake hold expiry (`checkAndRestorePreBrakeMode()`), if `SmartRadarLightEngine.isAutomationEnabled && !SmartRadarLightEngine.isManualOverridden`, the controller SHALL immediately dispatch the computed automatic target mode (`forceImmediate = true`); otherwise exact `preBrakeMode` restoration is preserved. `SmartRadarLightEngine` SHALL remove `@Synchronized` from `start`/`startListening`/`stopListening`/`stop` and use a pure in-memory leaf lock `engineLock` (never held across `SensorManager`, `scope.launch`, `Handler`, or `RadarLightController` calls), guaranteeing $E(L_A \to L_B) = E(L_B \to L_A) = \emptyset$ (`REQ-CON-056.4`); public `@JvmStatic` signatures remain unchanged; `RadarLightController.kt <= 1,500` LoC; `SmartRadarLightEngine.kt <= 1,500` LoC; `0` string/schema edits.

**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:
• *Given* a connected radar (non-StVZO or StVZO) outside a workout or during an active cycling workout (started in manual or auto mode), *When* the user selects a manual mode (`SOLID`/`PELOTON`/`DAY_FLASH`) and switches back to `Automatik` via `setAutoMode()` or `cycleNextMode()` (`OFF -> AUTO` + debounce flush), *Then* physical hardware and UI `currentMode` transition immediately to the computed automatic mode (`OFF`/`SOLID`/`DAY_FLASH` daytime; `PELOTON`/`SOLID`/`NIGHT_FLASH` night/tunnel; clamped to `OFF`/`SOLID` under StVZO) rather than remaining stuck on the prior manual mode.
• *Given* rapid multi-taps `OFF -> AUTO -> PELOTON -> SOLID` within 250 ms, *When* `cycleNextMode()` runs and the 400 ms debounce flushes, *Then* UI updates on each step and strictly 1 hardware write (`SOLID`) is transmitted.
• *Given* an active brake light during manual mode, *When* the user switches to `Automatik` and the 2.0 s post-brake hold expires, *Then* active brake light is never preempted, hold expiry immediately dispatches the computed automatic mode, and 5,000-iteration concurrent stress testing yields zero AB-BA deadlocks.

**Chesterton's Fence (`REQ-PRO-022`)**:
1. *Original Requirement ID & Target*: Net-new `REQ-CON-059` refining `REQ-CON-031.3`/`.8`, `REQ-CON-034.4`/`.5`, `REQ-CON-035.3`, `REQ-CON-047.3`, and `REQ-CON-056.2`/`.4` in `RadarLightController.kt` and `SmartRadarLightEngine.kt`.
2. *Historical Origin & Commit Trace*: `88ed2667` (`ATT-5104`), `1a6606d5` (`ATT-5121`), `c422e1b6` (`ATT-7991`), `5a1aafe0` (`ATT-8455`), `3c38a138` (`ATT-8132`), `8efc52af` (`ATT-8674`).
3. *Root Reason for Existing Formulation*: `1500 ms` rate-limiting and `400 ms` tap debouncing protect BLE/ANT+ queues; brake preemption and disjoint locking guarantee road safety and deadlock freedom.
4. *Preservation of Core Invariants*: Autonomous 1.5 s rate-limiting, 400 ms rapid-tap coalescing, StVZO pruning, night anti-glare cap, brake preemption + 2.0 s hold, offline gated mutation, external DataStore live propagation, and deadlock-free lock hierarchy all remain intact.
